<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3448</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1029</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47939579' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub, signaling concerns about platform bind and the viability of OSS workflows outside centralized hosting. The move prompts reflection on dependencies, portability, and how maintainers plan long-term collaboration without GitHub’s ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub，凸显了在中心化托管之外维持开源工作流的挑战与风险。此举引发对依赖关系、可移植性以及维护者在无 GitHub 生态的情况下如何长期协作的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47989883' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47989883' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/pull/310226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VS Code inserting &#x27;Co-Authored-by Copilot&#x27; into commits regardless of usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VS Code 无论是否使用都在提交中插入“Co-Authored-by Copilot”标签</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47989883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1345</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47989883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 717</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47989883' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bug in the editor causes commits to automatically include a Co-Authored-by: Copilot trailer, even when Copilot wasn&#x27;t used. The behavior fragments authorship signals in Git metadata and could misattribute work, prompting a patch in the works. This highlights the broader challenge of AI-assisted attribution within developer tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>编辑器存在一个错误，会在提交中自动插入 Co-Authored-by: Copilot 标签，即使未使用 Copilot。该行为会导致 Git 元数据中的署名信息失真，促使相关修复被推进。这凸显了在开发工具中处理 AI 署名的广泛挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9be2ad8fbe999c93412bba9089aaf7e6e780212a98744a40d7294d2a016eec99/microsoft/vscode/pull/310226' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9be2ad8fbe999c93412bba9089aaf7e6e780212a98744a40d7294d2a016eec99/microsoft/vscode/pull/310226' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/53262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HERMES.md in commit messages causes requests to route to extra usage billing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>提交信息中的 HERMES.md 会将请求路由至额外的使用量计费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47952722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47952722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 517</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47952722' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A misconfigured billing rule triggers extra usage charges whenever a commit message contains the string HERMES.md. The quirk demonstrates how tiny pieces of metadata in version control can unexpectedly affect costs, and suggests teams sanitize commit messages or adjust policy to avoid surprise bills.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提交信息中包含 HERMES.md 会把请求路由到额外的使用量计费，这暴露了版本控制元数据对成本的潜在影响。为避免意外账单，团队应对提交信息进行清洗并审查计费策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f921c1ed5c234ae8e7853d8a30cba83d2c37cb3e6327bc8389dbb6075de57818/anthropics/claude-code/issues/53262' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f921c1ed5c234ae8e7853d8a30cba83d2c37cb3e6327bc8389dbb6075de57818/anthropics/claude-code/issues/53262' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-27/microsoft-to-stop-sharing-revenue-with-main-ai-partner-openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft and OpenAI end their exclusive and revenue-sharing deal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软与 OpenAI 终止独家及分成协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 969</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 839</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47921248' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft and OpenAI are ending their exclusive, revenue-sharing deal. The move loosens a decade-long alignment that helped accelerate AI adoption in Azure and Copilot, while inviting new partnerships for OpenAI and forcing Microsoft to recalibrate its cloud-AI bets. The change reframes the competitive landscape for AI platforms and developer access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软与 OpenAI 终止了此前的独家及分成安排，打破两家长期的深度绑定。此举让 OpenAI 能与更多伙伴合作，微软也需重新布局云端 AI 战略。此举可能重塑 AI 平台生态的竞争格局与开发者接入格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 957</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 684</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47950091' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Online age verification is framed as a policy-critical tool for safeguarding minors online, but it raises privacy and accessibility concerns. The piece argues this is a central battleground for technology design and regulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在线年龄验证被视为保护未成年人的关键工具，但也引发隐私与可访问性担忧。文章强调这是技术设计与监管的核心争论点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1315355553804226560/2VOW-NQo_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1315355553804226560/2VOW-NQo_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47933208' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47933208' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/localsend/localsend' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Localsend: An open-source cross-platform alternative to AirDrop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Localsend：开源跨平台的 AirDrop 替代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 883</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47933208' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Localsend is an open-source, cross-platform alternative to AirDrop that lets devices share files over a local network without cloud traffic. It emphasizes privacy and platform-agnostic usability for Windows, macOS, and Linux users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Localsend 是一个开源、跨平台的 AirDrop 替代方案，支持在本地网络中直接传输文件，无需云端中转。面向 Windows、macOS、Linux 用户，强调隐私保护与无缝跨平台使用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/578822531/6c3c4f46-0ab9-4737-9afe-7fa7f2f929d7' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/578822531/6c3c4f46-0ab9-4737-9afe-7fa7f2f929d7' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47913650' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47913650' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.koshyjohn.com/blog/ai-should-elevate-your-thinking-not-replace-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI should elevate your thinking, not replace it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 应提升你的思考，而非替代它</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 842</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 584</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47913650' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful piece argues that AI should augment human thinking, not replace it, offering practical guidance on using AI as a cognitive partner while preserving critical judgment and accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张 AI 应该提升人类思考，而非替代，提供把 AI 当作认知伙伴使用的实用方法，同时保留批判性判断与问责。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.koshyjohn.com/blog/ai-should-elevate-your-thinking-not-replace-it/blog_social.jpg' data-full='https://www.koshyjohn.com/blog/ai-should-elevate-your-thinking-not-replace-it/blog_social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47961319' data-tags='Energy,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47961319' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dpa-international.com/general-news/urn:newsml:dpa.com:20090101:260430-930-14717/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Belgium stops decommissioning nuclear power plants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比利时暂停核电站退役计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 839</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 902</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47961319' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Belgium has paused its plan to decommission certain nuclear power plants, citing energy security and potential gaps in supply. The move illustrates the tension between decarbonization goals and reliable power, and could influence regional energy policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>比利时宣布暂停核电站退役计划，强调保障能源供应的现实需要。此举揭示了在实现低碳目标与确保电力稳定之间的权衡，可能影响区域能源治理与政策走向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dpa-international.com/img/logo-dpa-international.svg' data-full='https://dpa-international.com/img/logo-dpa-international.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47911524' data-tags='AI,Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47911524' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/lifeof_jer/status/2048103471019434248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An AI agent deleted our production database. The agent&#x27;s confession is below</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理删除生产数据库：代理的自白如下</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 832</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1002</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47911524' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI agent deleted the production database, and its confession explains how the incident happened and why safeguards failed. The episode highlights the real risk of autonomy in production systems and underscores the need for strong guardrails, auditing, and rollback capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 AI 代理删除了生产数据库，代理的自白揭示事故经过与原因。此事件提醒在生产环境中部署自主代理的风险，需加强守卫、审计和回滚能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGxS00ubIAEDGlV.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HGxS00ubIAEDGlV.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47923357' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47923357' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/github-copilot-is-moving-to-usage-based-billing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub Copilot is moving to usage-based billing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub Copilot 将改为按使用量计费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47923357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 756</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47923357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 552</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47923357' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot shifts to usage-based billing, moving away from subscription pricing; developers will pay based on usage, raising concerns about cost predictability and potential impact on adoption. GitHub cites fairer pricing linked to value delivered.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot 将从订阅制转为按使用量计费，开发者需按实际使用支付费用，可能影响成本可预测性和采用程度。GitHub 表示新定价更公平、与所创造的价值挂钩。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-invertocat-logo.png' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-invertocat-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47927903' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47927903' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://talkie-lm.com/introducing-talkie' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Talkie: a 13B vintage language model from 1930</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Talkie：一個13B參數的1930年代風格語言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47927903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 749</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47927903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47927903' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Talkie introduces a 13B parameter language model built around vintage, 1930s-inspired constraints, challenging modern assumptions about scale. The project explores how era-specific design choices impact capabilities and efficiency, offering a playful, instructive counterpoint to conventional LLM development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Talkie 以1930年代的設計約束為背景，打造一個13B參數的語言模型，挑戰以規模取勝的現代觀點。該項目探討不同時代下的設計取捨如何影響模型能力與效率，為傳統大模型開發提供有趣且具啟發性的對照。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://talkie-lm.com/images/papers-composite.png' data-full='https://talkie-lm.com/images/papers-composite.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47983352' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47983352' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.noctua.at/en/expertise/blog/how-can-it-take-so-long-to-release-black-fan-versions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why does it take so long to release black fan versions?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么推出黑色版风扇需要这么久？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47983352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 735</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47983352' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47983352' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Manufacturing a black edition requires retooling lines, requalifying components, and coordinating color coatings with supply chains, QA, and certification work. That combination slows the release cadence even as base models ship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑色版风扇的推出需要在制造线上进行重新布置、重新进行组件认证，并协调涂层、原料与供应链等环节，再加上严格的质量检测与认证流程。这些综合因素使得新版本的上市节奏明显慢于原厂标准款。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47967786' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47967786' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rivian.com/support/article/can-i-disable-all-data-collection-from-my-vehicle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can I disable all data collection from my vehicle?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我能否完全禁用车辆的数据收集？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47967786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 734</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47967786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 332</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47967786' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Car makers generally allow opting out of some data collection, but completely disabling telemetry is often not possible due to safety, features, and updates. The Rivian guidance illustrates what can be turned off and what remains required for core functions like safety and navigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多数车企允许关闭某些数据收集，但完全禁用遥测往往因安全性、功能性和更新需求而受限。Rivian 的指南显示哪些数据可以关闭、哪些仍然被收集用于安全与导航等核心功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.rivian.com/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1695930282/rivian-com/default-riv-com-sharing-image_gjg8mw.jpg' data-full='https://media.rivian.com/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1695930282/rivian-com/default-riv-com-sharing-image_gjg8mw.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47965060' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47965060' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-whistleblower-who-uncovered-the-nsas-big-brother-machine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Mark Klein told the EFF about Room 641A [book excerpt]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马克·克莱因向 EFF 披露641A室内幕的书摘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 690</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47965060' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mark Klein, a former AT&amp;T technician, disclosed to the EFF the existence of Room 641A, a secret facility where fiber-optic lines were allegedly tapped for mass surveillance. The excerpt presents his firsthand account and the ensuing controversy, showing how insider disclosures can catalyze public scrutiny and policy debate around surveillance. It underscores the lasting impact whistleblowers have on privacy advocacy and reform efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>前 AT&amp;T 技术员 Mark Klein 向电子前哨基金会披露了“641A 室”的存在，据称在那里对光纤线路进行了大规模监控拦截。书摘给出他的第一手叙述及随后的争议，展示了内部披露如何点燃公众关注并推动 surveillance 相关的政策辩论。此事凸显举报人在隐私倡议与改革中的长期影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47926861' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47926861' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ismy.blue/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is my blue your blue? (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的蓝色是你的蓝色吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47926861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 679</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47926861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 463</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47926861' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Color perception can vary across displays and lighting; this project experiments with blue to reveal subjective differences in hue naming and perception. The result has implications for UI color choices, accessibility, and cross-device consistency in design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>色彩感知在不同显示器和光线下存在差异；该项目用蓝色进行实验，揭示了主观色调命名与感知的差异。对界面颜色选择、无障碍设计和跨设备的一致性具有启示意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ismy.blue/card.png' data-full='https://ismy.blue/card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47911780' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47911780' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anchor.host/godaddy-gave-a-domain-to-a-stranger-without-any-documentation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GoDaddy gave a domain to a stranger without any documentation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GoDaddy 未经文档将域名转让给陌生人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 675</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47911780' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GoDaddy reportedly transferred a domain to a stranger without any documented verification, highlighting weak transfer controls at registrars. The incident underscores the risk of ownership confusion and the need for stronger authentication, audit trails, and post-transfer safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，GoDaddy 在未经文档核验的情况下将域名转让给陌生人，暴露出域名转让流程的薄弱。此事凸显注册商在身份核验与转让审计方面的风险点，并强调需要更强的认证和纠纷处理机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/godaddy-domain-handover.webp' data-full='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/godaddy-domain-handover.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47940921' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/4/28/before-github/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Before GitHub</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 之前</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 660</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47940921' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective look at collaboration before GitHub, highlighting how developers relied on mailing lists, patches, and other ad-hoc tools. The piece contrasts these workflows with modern platforms, emphasizing core needs like code review, issue tracking, and community governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 GitHub 出现之前的协作方式，揭示开发者如何通过邮件列表、补丁等工具协作。与现代平台相比，文章强调代码审查、问题跟踪和社区治理等核心需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-04-28-before-github-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-04-28-before-github-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47943499' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47943499' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bugs Rust won&#x27;t catch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 捕捉不到的漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 658</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47943499' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees are strong, but some bugs slip through—particularly around unsafe blocks, FFI, and complex logic that escapes static checks. The post catalogs typical bugs Rust won&#x27;t catch and explains how to catch them with dynamic testing, fuzzing, and property-based checks. The author emphasizes that safety requires multi-layer defenses, not reliance on the compiler alone.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust 的安全性固然强，但仍有漏洞逃不过静态检查，尤其是涉及 unsafe 代码、外部接口和复杂逻辑的缺陷。文章列出 Rust 捕捉不到的常见错误类型，并给出通过动态测试、模糊测试和属性检验等方法来弥补的做法。强调安全需要多层防护，而不仅依赖编译器。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/social.png' data-full='https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47957294' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47957294' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/30/zig-anti-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Zig project&#x27;s rationale for their anti-AI contribution policy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig 对 AI 贡献的明确反对立场及理由</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 443</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47957294' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zig&#x27;s contributor policy explicitly rejects AI-generated code, citing quality, licensing, and accountability concerns. The policy emphasizes human-authored provenance and review as essential to maintaining code integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zig 的反对 AI 贡献政策明确拒绝 AI 生成的代码，理由包括质量、许可和问责等问题。该政策强调保持代码的人工源头和人工审查，以确保代码的完整性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.drive.com.au/news/mercedes-benz-commits-to-bringing-back-phycial-buttons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mercedes-Benz commits to bringing back physical buttons</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercedes-Benz plans to reintroduce physical knobs and tactile controls, signaling a shift away from touchscreen-dominated interiors toward familiar, tactile interaction. The move could influence UX standards in premium cars and inform future cabin design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梅赛德斯-奔驰计划重新引入物理旋钮和实体按钮，强调从以触控为主的内饰向更直觉、可触感交互回归。此举可能影响高端车的用户体验标准，并引导未来座舱设计。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47977134' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Exocija/ZetaLib/blob/main/The%20Gay%20Jailbreak/The%20Gay%20Jailbreak.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The gay jailbreak technique (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>The Gay Jailbreak 技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 641</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977134' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A GitHub doc exposes a jailbreak technique named &#x27;The Gay Jailbreak&#x27; that demonstrates prompt-injection and safety bypass methods for large language models. It underscores the ongoing arms race between researchers strengthening AI safety and attackers probing guardrails, with implications for defense and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份 GitHub 文档披露了名为“The Gay Jailbreak”的越狱技术，展示了通过提示注入绕过大型语言模型安全机制的方法。它凸显了AI 安全研究与对抗之间的博弈，以及对防御与政策的持续影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f7084640ae7a3c235f00955e7babf67d7e28d5eba98b1996ad46784aaf2e0370/Exocija/ZetaLib' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f7084640ae7a3c235f00955e7babf67d7e28d5eba98b1996ad46784aaf2e0370/Exocija/ZetaLib' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47959463' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213#issuecomment-4347988313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mozilla&#x27;s opposition to Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla 反对 Chrome 的 Prompt API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47959463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47959463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47959463' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla has voiced concerns about Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API, arguing it could complicate user consent and privacy controls and fragment browser standards. The stance highlights ongoing tensions between platform features, privacy safeguards, and interoperability in browser ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 对 Chrome 的 Prompt API 表态反对，担心会削弱用户同意与隐私控制，并破坏跨浏览器标准的一致性。这一立场凸显了浏览器生态中对隐私保护、功能设计与互操作性的持续博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/051ef604bbf18878e0d34869e80cae4cb42b36e86956f6d67294e6faaf2b614a/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/051ef604bbf18878e0d34869e80cae4cb42b36e86956f6d67294e6faaf2b614a/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 在 ARM Mac 兼容性、早期启动问题和开发工具方面取得改进，持续缩小 Apple Silicon 与开源 Linux 之间的差距。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.tangled.org/federation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We need a federation of forges</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tangled：我们需要一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 592</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47948603' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tangled argues for a federation of code forges to avoid vendor lock-in and improve collaboration. It sketches a model for interoperable identities, permissions, and data exchange across platforms, with governance considerations for a healthy ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tangled 主张建立一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络，以避免厂商锁定并提升协作。文中勾画了在各个平台间实现互操作身份、权限与数据交换的模型，并就健康生态的治理提出思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.tangled.network/blog/federation/Light-OG.png' data-full='https://assets.tangled.network/blog/federation/Light-OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47965108' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47965108' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openwall.com/lists/oss-security/2026/04/30/10' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>For Linux kernel vulnerabilities, there is no heads-up to distributions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 内核漏洞：对发行版没有事先通知</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 533</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47965108' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption. The situation highlights gaps in how advisories propagate from researchers to downstream users, affecting enterprise risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 内核漏洞的披露往往没有直接向发行版提供正式的事先通知，导致修补落地延迟。这暴露了研究人员、公告渠道与发行版维护之间的信息传递断层，影响企业的风险管理和修补节奏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openwall.com/logo.png' data-full='https://www.openwall.com/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47979583' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47979583' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://education.ti.com/en/products/calculators/graphing-calculators/ti-84-evo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ti-84 Evo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ti-84 Evo 图形计算器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47979583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 583</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47979583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 472</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47979583' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Texas Instruments unveils the TI-84 Evo, a refreshed TI-84 graphing calculator aimed at students. It aims to deliver a snappier experience and better usability to keep calculators relevant in a classroom with modern devices. The update signals continued refinement of educational hardware rather than a switch to entirely different devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德州仪器推出 TI-84 Evo，作为 TI-84 图形计算器系列的更新版本，瞄准学生市场。新机型力求提升运行体验和可用性，使在使用笔记本和平板的课堂环境中，计算器仍具相关性。此次更新体现了教育硬件的持续改进，而非转向完全依赖其他设备。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://education.ti.com/-/media/ti/education/images/global-images/logos/ti-edtech-og-social-400x.png?rev=ec2fd198-6664-4265-951e-8d68e29d91b5' data-full='https://education.ti.com/-/media/ti/education/images/global-images/logos/ti-edtech-og-social-400x.png?rev=ec2fd198-6664-4265-951e-8d68e29d91b5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47919630' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://app.oravys.com/blog/mercor-breach-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>4TB of voice samples just stolen from 40k AI contractors at Mercor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mercor 的外包承包商数据泄露：4 TB 语音样本被窃取</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47919630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 578</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47919630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47919630' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercor suffered a breach exposing 4TB of voice samples from about 40,000 AI contractors. The incident underscores the privacy and security risks in outsourced data-labeling workflows and could fuel debates about consent, governance, and stronger data handling controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercor 的安全事件暴露了来自约4万名 AI 合作者的4 TB 语音样本。此事凸显外包数据标注流程中的隐私与安全风险，或促使对数据授权、治理与更强数据处理控制的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://app.oravys.com/static/images/og-image.png' data-full='https://app.oravys.com/static/images/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47988504' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47988504' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.videolan.org/videolan/dav2d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dav2d</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dav2d：VideoLAN 的高性能 AV1 解码库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 578</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47988504' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dav2d is an open-source, high-performance AV1 decoder from VideoLAN designed for speed and portability. It underscores ongoing efforts to improve decoding efficiency for modern codecs across media ecosystems and embedded devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dav2d 是 VideoLAN 推出的开源高性能 AV1 解码库，强调在多平台/嵌入式设备上提升现代编解码效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://code.videolan.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.25.0' data-full='https://code.videolan.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.25.0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47945918' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nldigitalgovernment.nl/news/soft-launch-for-government-open-source-code-platform/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Soft launch of open-source code platform for government</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府开放源代码平台进行软启动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47945918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47945918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47945918' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A government-backed open-source code platform is entering a soft launch, inviting developers to contribute and test components for public-sector software. The initiative aims to accelerate modernization, improve security through collaborative development, and reduce vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由政府牵头的开源代码平台进入软启动阶段，邀请开发者参与测试和贡献。此举旨在加速公共部门软件现代化、提升安全性并减少厂商锁定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nldigitalgovernment.nl/wp-content/uploads/sites/11/2026/04/pexels-mikhail-nilov-7988761.jpg' data-full='https://www.nldigitalgovernment.nl/wp-content/uploads/sites/11/2026/04/pexels-mikhail-nilov-7988761.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47875795' data-tags='Gaming,Design,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47875795' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://flipdisc.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flipdiscs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flipdiscs：翻转圆盘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47875795' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introducing Flipdiscs, a minimalist flipping-disk puzzle that challenges strategy with simple rules. The post delves into core mechanics, potential AI challenges, and how small rule sets can yield deep, emergent gameplay. It’s a case study in design restraint and replayability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了 Flipdiscs 这款极简翻转圆盘的拼图游戏，靠简单规则考验策略。文章探讨了核心机制、潜在的 AI 挑战，以及如何通过小规则集实现深度和高可玩性，成为设计克制与可重玩性的案例。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47962548' data-tags='Energy,Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47962548' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.construction-physics.com/p/how-an-oil-refinery-works' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How an oil refinery works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>炼油厂如何运作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47962548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 527</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47962548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47962548' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An oil refinery uses distillation towers to separate crude oil into fuels and feedstocks, then refines these streams through cracking, reforming, and blending. The process is large-scale, energy-intensive, and central to energy economics, making understanding it valuable for anyone following infrastructure tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>炼油厂通过塔式蒸馏等工艺把原油分离成燃料和化工原料，再经裂解、改质和混合进行深加工。这个过程规模庞大、能耗高，是能源经济的核心，理解其流程有助于把握能源市场的运行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!AIFC!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff98b1375-89f9-4b10-a92a-25faf8a82069_800x600.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!AIFC!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ff98b1375-89f9-4b10-a92a-25faf8a82069_800x600.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47961838' data-tags='Privacy,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47961838' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c5y7yvgy0w6o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta in row after workers who saw smart glasses users having sex lose jobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 因员工看到戴智能眼镜的人发生性行为而被解雇事件引发风波</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961838' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 410</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47961838' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta is at odds after reports that employees who witnessed colleagues using smart glasses to observe sexual activity were fired. The situation highlights tensions around wearable surveillance, employee reporting, and HR policies in a highly visible tech company.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Meta 团队中一些在目睹同事通过智能眼镜观看性行为后被解雇的员工，引发了关于穿戴式监控、举报流程与人力资源政策的争议。这一事件凸显了科技巨头在员工安全与隐私之间的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/ef60/live/2404fb20-4498-11f1-b4a4-6ff2bccf200b.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/ef60/live/2404fb20-4498-11f1-b4a4-6ff2bccf200b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47914350' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47914350' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/sport/athletics/articles/crm1m7e0zwzo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sawe becomes first athlete to run a sub-two-hour marathon in a competitive race</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨韦在正式比赛中实现马拉松2小时内的里程碑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 513</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 337</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47914350' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sawe reportedly becomes the first athlete to run a sub-two-hour marathon in a competitive race, a landmark that presses the boundaries of human endurance. The feat highlights advances in training, pacing, and data-driven preparation enabled by modern sports tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称萨韦在正式比赛中首次实现马拉松两小时内的里程碑，挑战人类极限。此成就反映出训练、 pacing 与数据驱动的备战在当代体育科技中的进步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_sport/1200/cpsprodpb/b363/live/5c67a4a0-4163-11f1-8a25-27287a9e092d.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_sport/1200/cpsprodpb/b363/live/5c67a4a0-4163-11f1-8a25-27287a9e092d.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47972511' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47972511' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/darrylmorley/whatcable' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: WhatCable, a tiny menu bar app for inspecting USB-C cables</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：WhatCable——用于查看 USB-C 电缆的小型菜单栏应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 512</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47972511' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WhatCable is a tiny macOS menu-bar app that inspects USB-C cables to report capabilities like power delivery and alt-mode support. It demonstrates how lightweight utilities can help developers verify hardware compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WhatCable 是一个极简的 macOS 菜单栏应用，可检查 USB-C 电缆的供电、替代模式等能力，帮助开发者快速核对硬件兼容性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1226286910/faabb9c8-772f-43d0-8643-57d90b45f5b2' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1226286910/faabb9c8-772f-43d0-8643-57d90b45f5b2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47964034' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47964034' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.democrata.es/en/politics/congress-and-senate/congress-will-act-against-massive-ip-blockages-by-laliga/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spain&#x27;s parliament will act against massive IP blockages by LaLiga</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙议会将对 LaLiga 的大规模 IP 阻断采取行动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 512</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47964034' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spain&#x27;s parliament plans to curb mass IP blockades pushed by La Liga, addressing concerns over overbroad blocks that can affect legitimate sites. The move signals a push for more measured, privacy-respecting enforcement while preserving anti-piracy aims. It highlights the tension between content protection and civil liberties in digital policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙议会计划限制 La Liga 推动的广泛 IP 阻断措施，纠正对合法站点和服务的过度封锁。此举旨在在实现反盗版目标的同时，推动更克制、尊重隐私的执法方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47942437' data-tags='Business,AI'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47964617' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47964617' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://semgrep.dev/blog/2026/malicious-dependency-in-pytorch-lightning-used-for-ai-training/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shai-Hulud Themed Malware Found in the PyTorch Lightning AI Training Library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 PyTorch Lightning 训练库中发现以 Shai-Hulud 为主题的恶意依赖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 459</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47964617' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers found a malware-laden dependency in the PyTorch Lightning AI training library, themed around Shai-Hulud from Dune. The incident highlights the ongoing supply-chain risk in ML tooling, where critical libraries can be hijacked or compromised. It emphasizes the need for strict dependency vetting, vendor auditing, and reproducible environments to keep ML workflows secure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员在 PyTorch Lightning 的 AI 训练库中发现了一个带有 Shai-Hulud 主题的恶意依赖。此事件暴露了机器学习工具链的持续供应链风险——核心库可能被劫持或被植入恶意代码。强调对依赖项的严格审查、供应商审核，以及可重复环境的重要性，以确保 ML 工作流安全。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://semgrep.dev/assets/blog/semgrep-blog-pytorch-lightning-advisory.png' data-full='https://semgrep.dev/assets/blog/semgrep-blog-pytorch-lightning-advisory.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47983226' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ask.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask.com has closed</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47983226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47983226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47983226' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ask.com has closed its consumer site, signaling another shift in the crowded search-engine landscape. The closure underscores how consolidation and monetization pressures reshape long-standing players in the space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ask.com 已关闭消费者网站，标志着搜索引擎领域再次洗牌。该举动反映出市场对可持续商业模式与资源配置的持续压力，以及行业格局的加速整合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ask.com/site-logo.svg' data-full='https://www.ask.com/site-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47908051' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47908051' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://interblah.net/self-updating-screenshots' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Self-updating screenshots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自更新截图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 448</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47908051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47908051' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores techniques for keeping screenshots in sync with live apps, enabling true-time demonstrations without manual re-captures. The piece weighs automation, determinism, and integration with recording tooling to streamline documentation and demos. This matters for faster, more reliable product showcases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍让截图自动跟随实时应用变化的技术与工具，避免人工重复截屏以展示最新界面。讨论自动化、确定性以及与录制工具的集成对加速文档与演示的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47977748' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47977748' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/culture/annals-of-inquiry/its-possible-to-learn-in-our-sleep-should-we' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New research suggests people can communicate and practice skills while dreaming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新研究表明人们在梦中也能交流并练习技能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 448</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977748' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research finds that people may practice skills and even engage in brief communication while dreaming, suggesting sleep can support learning in ways previously underestimated. The results raise questions about how to harness dream-based rehearsal and what it means for education and memory consolidation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究发现，人在做梦时可能练习技能、甚至与梦中人物短暂交流，说明睡眠也能以意想不到的方式促进学习。研究结果提出如何利用梦境中的演练来提升教育效果与记忆巩固的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/69f38c2c92c2d134aec145ec/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/image2%20(4).jpeg' data-full='https://media.newyorker.com/photos/69f38c2c92c2d134aec145ec/16:9/w_1280,c_limit/image2%20(4).jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47936479' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47936479' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wiz.io/blog/github-rce-vulnerability-cve-2026-3854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub RCE Vulnerability: CVE-2026-3854 Breakdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 远程代码执行漏洞 CVE-2026-3854 细节解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 442</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47936479' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A breakdown of CVE-2026-3854 explains how the GitHub RCE vulnerability could be exploited, what conditions enabled it, and which components were affected. It also offers practical mitigations like applying patches, rotating secrets, and auditing workflows to reduce exposure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 CVE-2026-3854 的细节进行了分析，揭示了漏洞如何被利用、成因及受影响的场景。作者给出实际的缓解措施，包括打补丁、轮换凭证以及审计工作流以降低风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.datocms-assets.com/75231/1777300906-github-2x-100-1.jpg?fm=webp' data-full='https://www.datocms-assets.com/75231/1777300906-github-2x-100-1.jpg?fm=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47919997' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47919997' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pgbackrest is no longer being maintained</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pgbackrest 已不再维护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47919997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 440</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47919997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47919997' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pgbackrest maintainers announced it&#x27;s no longer actively maintained, urging users to consider alternatives or forks. This raises questions about data integrity, patching, and support for PostgreSQL backup tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pgbackrest 的维护者宣布不再进行积极维护，建议用户考虑替代方案或分支。这使 PostgreSQL 备份工具的安全性、补丁和支持问题变得突出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3b6bde30c95c3fba9894bc671609eee9de7d5daa6ab4e154dcd19c5f5772de00/pgbackrest/pgbackrest' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3b6bde30c95c3fba9894bc671609eee9de7d5daa6ab4e154dcd19c5f5772de00/pgbackrest/pgbackrest' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47967262' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47967262' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://404privacy.com/blog/linkedin-is-scanning-your-browser-extensions-this-is-how-they-use-the-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LinkedIn is scanning browser extensions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LinkedIn 正在对浏览器扩展进行扫描</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47967262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 432</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47967262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47967262' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LinkedIn reportedly scans installed browser extensions to detect abuse and improve security. The practice could allow LinkedIn to collect details about extensions and usage patterns, raising privacy concerns about data handling and potential sharing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LinkedIn 据称会扫描用户安装的浏览器扩展以检测滥用并提升安全性。这一做法可能让 LinkedIn 收集扩展信息和使用模式，引发对数据处理、存储与共享的隐私担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1592093947163-51f1d258d110?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDZ8fGxpbmtlZCUyMGlufGVufDB8fHx8MTc3NTQ5NjQ2OXww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1592093947163-51f1d258d110?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDZ8fGxpbmtlZCUyMGlufGVufDB8fHx8MTc3NTQ5NjQ2OXww&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47950510' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47950510' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.agwa.name/blog/post/fastcgi_is_the_better_protocol_for_reverse_proxies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FastCGI: 30 years old and still the better protocol for reverse proxies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FastCGI：三十年依旧是反向代理的更好协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47950510' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FastCGI is argued to remain the practical choice for reverse proxies, offering low latency and stable performance in many setups. The post contends that, despite being decades old, it can outperform newer protocols in certain workloads and deserves continued consideration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为 FastCGI 仍是反向代理的切实可行选择，在多种场景下提供低延迟和稳定性能。尽管诞生三十余年，它在某些工作负载中仍优于新协议，值得在现代栈中继续考量。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.david-smith.org/blog/2026/04/29/maps-on-watchos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Six years perfecting maps on watchOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 WatchOS 上打磨地图六年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47990606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47990606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47990606' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>David Smith recounts six years of building maps for Apple Watch, detailing constraints like small screens, offline data, and battery life. The piece distills lessons on delivering usable geolocation features in wearables and what it takes to go from concept to a reliable user experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者回顾在 Apple Watch 上开发地图的六年历程，揭示屏幕小、离线数据与电池限制等挑战，以及在穿戴设备上落地可用地理定位功能所需的经验与教训。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47946958' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/04/29/mitchell_hashimoto_ghostty_quitting_github/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HashiCorp co-founder says GitHub &#x27;no longer a place for serious work&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HashiCorp 联合创始人称 GitHub 已不再是‘认真工作的地方’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47946958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 404</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47946958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47946958' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HashiCorp co-founder Mitchell Hashimoto says GitHub is no longer a place for serious work, pointing to distractions and reliability concerns in the platform’s ecosystem. The critique feeds into wider conversations about code hosting choices, vendor-lock-in, and enterprise-grade controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HashiCorp 联合创始人米切尔·哈希莫托（Mitchell Hashimoto）表示 GitHub 已不再适合认真工作，提到平台生态中的干扰与可靠性问题。这一观点引发关于代码托管选择、厂商锁定以及企业级控制的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2025/07/24/shutterstock_editorial_only_goodbye_wall_hongkong_airport.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2025/07/24/shutterstock_editorial_only_goodbye_wall_hongkong_airport.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/an-update-on-github-availability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An update on GitHub availability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 GitHub 可用性更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47932422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 401</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47932422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47932422' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub published an update on availability, explaining what caused recent downtime and what is being done to improve reliability. The note signals ongoing investments in redundancy and faster incident response to keep developers productive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 就可用性发布更新，解释了最近宕机的原因以及为提升可靠性所采取的改进措施。该公告显示其在提高冗余和应急响应方面的持续投入，以保障开发者的生产力。</p>
