<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47977383' data-tags='Energy,Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47977383' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://californiawaterblog.com/2026/04/26/ai-water-use-distractions-and-lessons-for-california/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI uses less water than the public thinks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能用水量低于公众预期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 377</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977383' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A California Water Blog post argues AI water use is smaller than common perception, despite AI&#x27;s energy footprint. It dives into cooling practices, water recycling, and regional policy implications for water-scarce states, urging nuance in water-footprint accounting as AI expands.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州水资源博客指出，尽管 AI 能耗很高，人工智能的数据中心用水量往往低于公众想象。文章探讨制冷方法、水循环再利用以及对水资源紧张地区的政策影响，强调在 AI 规模扩大时需要更精细的用水足迹核算。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/californiawaterblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Christine-ICON-circle-WaterBlog-Profile.png?fit=1625%2C1850&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/californiawaterblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Christine-ICON-circle-WaterBlog-Profile.png?fit=1625%2C1850&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47976415' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47976415' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.briefs.co/news/uber-torches-entire-2026-ai-budget-on-claude-code-in-four-months/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uber torches 2026 AI budget on Claude Code in four months</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>优步在四个月内将2026年的AI预算全数投向 Claude Code</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47976415' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47976415' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 469</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47976415' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Uber dramatically front-loads its AI investment, dedicating the entire 2026 budget to Claude Code for four months. This signals a push to accelerate internal AI-assisted coding, potentially reshaping its software-dev workflows and cost structure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>优步将在四个月内把2026年的AI预算全部投向Claude Code，提前放大对AI辅助编码的投入。这举措旨在加速内部开发流程，但也意味着成本压力和对 Claude Code 的高度依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.briefs.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/uber-torches-entire-2026-ai-budget-on-claude-code-in-four-months.png' data-full='https://www.briefs.co/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/uber-torches-entire-2026-ai-budget-on-claude-code-in-four-months.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok 4.3 是 Grok 模型家族的最新版本，为在该平台上构建应用的开发者带来 API 与能力的更新。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://worksinprogress.co/issue/the-worlds-most-complex-machine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ASML became the chokepoint for cutting-edge chips</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ASML 成为尖端芯片的瓶颈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47901064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47901064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47901064' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ASML&#x27;s lithography equipment remains the choke point for mass-producing leading-edge chips, driven by extreme complexity and supply chain constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>光刻机是当今顶尖芯片量产的瓶颈，受制于设备极高的复杂性和供应链挑战。分析涉及摩尔定律的前景、产业链影响与晶圆厂产能。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47920315' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/04/27/meta-manus-china-blocks-acquisition-ai-startup.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>China blocks Meta&#x27;s acquisition of AI startup Manus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>中国阻止 Meta 收购 Manus AI 初创公司</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47920315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 380</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47920315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47920315' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>China blocked Meta&#x27;s acquisition of AI startup Manus, signaling regulatory pushback on AI consolidation and cross-border deals. The decision underscores how different jurisdictions balance antitrust, national security, and tech leadership in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中国阻止 Meta 收购 Manus AI 初创公司，显示对 AI 行业整合及跨境交易的监管态度。此举凸显各地在反垄断、国家安全与人工智能领域领导力之间的博弈。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47933236' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47933236' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/microsoft/VibeVoice' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VibeVoice: Open-source frontier voice AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Microsoft VibeVoice：开源前沿的语音AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47933236' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft unveils VibeVoice as an open-source frontier for voice AI, providing models and tooling to build real-time speech apps. The move could accelerate community-driven innovation in voice tech while prompting questions about licensing, safety, and governance in open-source AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软推出 VibeVoice，作为开源语音AI的前沿项目，提供模型与工具用于构建实时语音应用。此举有望加速社区驱动的语音技术创新，同时引发关于许可、安全与治理的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5479ff32185f4ebce5cd228a5507eb9a8e7a6f57cf568fdec179cf0dc86c994b/microsoft/VibeVoice' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5479ff32185f4ebce5cd228a5507eb9a8e7a6f57cf568fdec179cf0dc86c994b/microsoft/VibeVoice' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47932990' data-tags='Privacy,Business,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47932990' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://femtechdesigndesk.substack.com/p/your-period-tracking-app-has-been' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Period tracking app, Flo, found to be selling user data to Meta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生理期追踪应用 Flo 被发现向 Meta 出售用户数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47932990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 376</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47932990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47932990' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flo, a popular period-tracking app, was found to sell user data to Meta. This highlights privacy risks in health apps and the blurred line between product features and data monetization, prompting calls for clearer consent, data minimization, and disclosures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>广受欢迎的生理期跟踪应用 Flo 被曝向 Meta 出售用户数据。此事暴露了健康类应用的隐私风险以及数据商业化的模糊界限，促使人们呼吁加强同意机制、数据最小化以及更清晰的数据使用披露。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47953541' data-tags='Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47953541' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opentrafficmap.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenTrafficMap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenTrafficMap 开放交通地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47953541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 375</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47953541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47953541' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenTrafficMap crowdsources and hosts open traffic data to create a free, open-access map of road conditions. By emphasizing openness and community contributions, it offers an alternative to proprietary map datasets and can power free APIs and dashboards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenTrafficMap 倡导开放数据和社区贡献，汇总交通信息并提供免费可访问的地图。它为替代私有地图数据集的解决方案，并可支持开放 API 与数据可视化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47953275' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47953275' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jivx.com/kyoto-bloom' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kyoto cherry blossoms now bloom earlier than at any point in 1,200 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>京都樱花比1200年以来的任何时候都早盛开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47953275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47953275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47953275' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kyoto&#x27;s cherry blossoms are blooming earlier than any time in 1,200 years, underscoring rapid phenology shifts linked to climate change. The trend affects tourism planning around peak bloom and raises questions about regional adaptation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>京都樱花开花时间比 1200 年来任何时刻都更早，反映出与气候变化相关的物候期变化。此趋势会影响樱花季的旅游安排，并引发对区域适应策略的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47973378' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47973378' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://x.com/aaronp613/status/2049986504617820551' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple accidentally left Claude.md files Apple Support app</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果在 Apple Support 应用中无意暴露 Claude.md 文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47973378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47973378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 311</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47973378' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A misconfiguration exposed Claude.md files in the Apple Support app. The files could reveal sensitive information about Claude integration or internal workflows, highlighting how even large platforms can leak developer docs if not careful.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个错误配置让 Apple Support 应用中暴露了 Claude.md 文件，可能泄露关于 Claude 集成的内部文档和工作流信息。这再次暴露了大型应用中的隐私与安全漏洞，以及加强访问控制的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HHMDhzqXEAATAX9.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HHMDhzqXEAATAX9.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47931035' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47931035' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gtfobins.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GTFOBins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GTFOBins：可滥用的二进制文件大全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47931035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 368</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47931035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47931035' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GTFOBins catalogs common Unix binaries that can be abused to bypass restrictions or escalate privileges, with practical usage examples. It serves as a practical reference for both attackers and defenders to understand potential attack paths. Keeping track of these binaries helps incident responders design better mitigations and detection rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GTFOBins 收集了常见的 Unix 二进制程序及其被滥用的场景，帮助利用这些工具进行提权、规避限制等。该资源为安全研究人员、红队与防守方提供实际的攻击面参考。持续关注这些条目有助于制定更有效的防护和检测策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gtfobins.org/assets/logo.png' data-full='https://gtfobins.org/assets/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47936264' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47936264' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.warp.dev/blog/warp-is-now-open-source' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Warp is now open-source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Warp 现已开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 364</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47936264' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Warp has become open-source, inviting community contributions and potential performance improvements for its GPU-accelerated terminal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Warp 的代码已开源，欢迎社区贡献，可能推动 GPU 加速终端的功能迭代与新集成。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.warp.dev/images/blog/warp-is-now-open-source/hero.png' data-full='https://www.warp.dev/images/blog/warp-is-now-open-source/hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47922712' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techzine.eu/news/infrastructure/140634/dutch-central-bank-chooses-lidl-for-european-cloud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dutch central bank ditches AWS and chooses Lidl for European Cloud</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰央行弃用 AWS，选择 Lidl 搭建欧洲云</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47922712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47922712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47922712' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netherlands central bank replaces AWS with Lidl for its European Cloud initiative, signaling a move toward EU-controlled cloud services. The decision highlights a broader push for data sovereignty and diversification away from US hyperscalers in regulated sectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰央行放弃 AWS，转用 Lidl 的欧洲云计划，体现对 EU 受控云服务的偏好与数据主权的考量。此举反映在金融等受监管行业中加强对云供应商多元化及本地化的趋势。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://artemistimeline.com/#artemis-ii-walkout-nhq202604010003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II Photo Timeline</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47954394' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA releases a photo timeline documenting Artemis II walkthrough and milestones, offering a visual diary of the mission&#x27;s path to a crewed lunar flyby. The sequence helps readers track preparation, hardware, and public-facing milestones as NASA advances human exploration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NASA 发布阿尔忒弥斯II 的照片时间线，记录从准备到载人绕月飞行等关键节点与工作流程。通过一组可视化材料，公众能直观看到任务进展、硬件进展以及对人类月球探测计划的阶段性里程碑。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47924912' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47924912' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2026/04/27/you-can-beat-the-binary-search/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can beat the binary search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你可以超越二分查找</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47924912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 343</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47924912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47924912' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows that under certain data distributions or with specialized techniques such as interpolation search, you can outperform classic binary search.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出在特定数据分布或条件下，类似插值查找等替代方法可超越二分查找，讨论权衡、缓存行为以及在实际场景中的适用性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lemire.me/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Gemini_Generated_Image_rsv3zvrsv3zvrsv3-1024x572.png' data-full='https://lemire.me/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Gemini_Generated_Image_rsv3zvrsv3zvrsv3-1024x572.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47903541' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47903541' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jordanlord.co.uk/blog/3-constraints/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three constraints before I build anything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在动手前的三个约束</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47903541' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates three non-negotiables before starting any build: define the core problem, constrain scope, and set measurable success criteria. The framework helps prevent scope creep and accelerates delivery by focusing on what truly matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在动手前，作者提出三个不可谈判的约束：明确核心问题、限定范围、设定可衡量的成功标准。通过聚焦要点，这一框架有助于防止范围蔓延并加速落地。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47913855' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47913855' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sentinelone.com/labs/fast16-mystery-shadowbrokers-reference-reveals-high-precision-software-sabotage-5-years-before-stuxnet/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fast16: High-precision software sabotage 5 years before Stuxnet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fast16：比 Stuxnet 早5年的高精度软件破坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913855' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913855' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47913855' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New evidence ties Fast16 to a high-precision sabotage capability years before Stuxnet, suggesting sophisticated cyber-physical attack capabilities existed well ahead of that famous incident. The finding underscores long-running threats to industrial control systems and the need for deeper defense in supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新证据将 Fast16 与比 Stuxnet 早现的高精度破坏能力联系起来，表明对工业控制系统的高端攻击能力早已存在。此发现凸显对供应链与控制网络的长期威胁，以及更深层防御的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sentinelone.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/fast16_3.jpg' data-full='https://www.sentinelone.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/fast16_3.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47910388' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47910388' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/why-we-no-longer-evaluate-swe-bench-verified/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SWE-bench Verified no longer measures frontier coding capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SWE-bench Verified 不再衡量前沿编码能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47910388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 339</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47910388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47910388' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SWE-bench Verified no longer measures frontier coding capabilities because the metric diverges from real-world coding tasks and can be gamed. The change signals a shift toward more robust, meaningful benchmarks for AI-assisted coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SWE-bench Verified 不再用于衡量前沿编码能力，因为该指标与实际编码任务脱节且易被操控。此举推动评测向更稳健、具备实际意义的基准转变，影响 AI 辅助编码工具的评估方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47965503' data-tags='Programming,Gaming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47965503' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nickkossolapov.github.io/fame-boy/building-a-game-boy-emulator-in-fsharp/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built a Game Boy emulator in F#</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用 F# 构建了一个 Game Boy 模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965503' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965503' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47965503' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I built a Game Boy emulator in F# from scratch, tackling the platform&#x27;s timing, CPU, and memory quirks with a functional language. The project showcases how expressive, type-safe FP languages can still perform low-level emulation, and offers insights on bridging game architecture with modern tooling. It&#x27;s a proof-of-concept that highlights the trade-offs between expressiveness and performance in hobby hardware emulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者用 F# 从零开始实现了一个 Game Boy 模拟器，需处理时序、CPU 与内存等细节。这个项目展示了函数式语言在底层仿真中的可行性与权衡，给出在现代工具链下进行硬件仿真的经验与启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nickkossolapov.github.io/fame-boy/building-a-game-boy-emulator-in-fsharp/images/pokemon.gif' data-full='https://nickkossolapov.github.io/fame-boy/building-a-game-boy-emulator-in-fsharp/images/pokemon.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47991981' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47991981' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/apr/30/ai-outperforms-doctors-in-harvard-trial-of-emergency-triage-diagnoses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI&#x27;s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients vs. 50-55% by triage doctors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 的 o1 在急诊诊断中正确率达 67%，超出分诊医生的 50–55%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47991981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47991981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47991981' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a Harvard trial, OpenAI&#x27;s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients, compared with 50-55% by triage doctors. This demonstrates AI&#x27;s potential to augment emergency triage, but real-world deployment must address safety, workflow integration, and clinician oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在哈佛的一项试验中，o1 在急诊患者诊断中的正确率达到 67%，明显高于分诊医生的 50–55%。这显示人工智能有潜力提升急诊分诊的效率与准确性，但将其落地需解决安全性、工作流整合以及医生监督等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7f4b1062db43fb053f4d68c08b8e92dd39f49107/0_62_4362_3487/master/4362.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=ceb8f56ddb65e715896d86461e95819f' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7f4b1062db43fb053f4d68c08b8e92dd39f49107/0_62_4362_3487/master/4362.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=ceb8f56ddb65e715896d86461e95819f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47951137' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lawsofux.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Laws of UX</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47951137' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Laws of UX bundles practical design heuristics into a navigable reference for product teams, covering interaction timing, visual cues, and cognitive load. It serves as a quick checklist to avoid common UX pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《UX 法则》汇集可操作的设计启发式，方便产品团队快速查阅，涵盖交互时序、视觉线索与认知负荷等要点，帮助避免常见的 UX 错误。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47934120' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47934120' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/markets/commodities/uae-says-it-quits-opec-opec-statement-2026-04-28/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UAE Leaves OPEC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿联酋退出欧佩克</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47934120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47934120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47934120' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The UAE has said it quits OPEC, removing a key member from the cartel. The move could give the UAE greater freedom over its oil production and price setting, while reordering OPEC&#x27;s influence in global markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿联酋宣布退出欧佩克，意味着该国将脱离石油卡特尔的成员身份。此举可能为阿布扎比在原油产量与定价方面带来更大自主权，同时重新调整欧佩克在全球市场的影响力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47951007' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47951007' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/apr/29/maryland-grocery-stores-ban-surveillance-pricing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maryland becomes first state to ban surveillance pricing in grocery stores</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马里兰成为首个禁止超市使用监控定价的州</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47951007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47951007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47951007' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Maryland becomes the first state to ban surveillance-based price discrimination in groceries, prohibiting price discrimination based on customer data. The move highlights growing policy attention to data-driven pricing and consumer privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马里兰成为首个禁止超市使用监控定价的州，禁止基于顾客数据的价格歧视做法。这一举措凸显对数据驱动定价与消费者隐私的日益关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/1a943d9c427bcfd9d46c95d09bfcd3de5938eb0d/611_1008_4879_3904/master/4879.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=fbcfe0780d6ffaf273637e888c420666' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/1a943d9c427bcfd9d46c95d09bfcd3de5938eb0d/611_1008_4879_3904/master/4879.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=fbcfe0780d6ffaf273637e888c420666' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47957101' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47957101' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jcvi.org/media-center/j-craig-venter-genomics-pioneer-and-founder-jcvi-and-diploid-genomics-inc-dies-79' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Craig Venter has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>克雷格·文特纳辞世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 331</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47957101' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Renowned genomics pioneer Craig Venter has died at 79. His work accelerating whole-genome sequencing and founding JCVI helped reshape biomedical research and the race toward personalized medicine.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球知名的基因组学先驱克雷格·文特纳去世，享年79岁。他推动整基因组测序的成本下降与速度提升，并创立 JCVI，深刻影响了生物医学研究和个性化医疗的发展。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47993235' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47993235' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thinkpol.ca/2026/04/30/an-open-weights-chinese-model-just-beat-claude-gpt-5-5-and-gemini-in-a-programming-challenge/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K2.6 just beat Claude, GPT-5.5, and Gemini in a coding challenge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K2.6 在编码挑战中击败 Claude、GPT-5.5 与 Gemini</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47993235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47993235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47993235' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K2.6, an open-weights Chinese model, beat Claude, GPT-5.5, and Gemini in a programming challenge. This underscores rapid progress in open-weight models and code-aware capabilities, showing non-proprietary models can compete with leading closed models on real tasks. It may heighten interest in open-source NLP tooling and benchmarks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi K2.6 在一次编码挑战中击败 Claude、GPT-5.5 与 Gemini，凸显开放权重模型在代码任务上的竞争力。此成就强调代码生成与理解领域的快速进步，以及对闭源大模型市场的潜在冲击。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thinkpol.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/tp_cover.png' data-full='https://thinkpol.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/tp_cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>致我的学生</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47928828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47928828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47987256' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47987256' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Self-preferencing in Algorithmic Hiring: Empirical Evidence and Insights</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>算法招聘中的AI自我偏好：实证证据与见解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47987256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47987256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47987256' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New empirical evidence shows hiring algorithms can self-preference, reinforcing existing biases by favoring candidates similar to those previously selected. The study highlights how model priors, training data, and feature design can create feedback loops, skewing outcomes even without explicit bias. Practical recommendations include auditing data, promoting diversity, and ensuring human oversight in selection decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实证研究显示招聘算法可能自我偏好，通过偏向与先前被选中的候选人相似的特征来强化现有偏见。研究指出模型先验、训练数据与特征设计会形成反馈回路，即使没有明确的偏见也会扭曲结果。建议包括审计数据、促进多样性，以及在筛选决策中确保人类监督。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47924775' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47924775' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub is having issues now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 现在出现故障</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47924775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47924775' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47924775' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub is experiencing an outage with degraded performance across core services. The disruption underscores how essential code hosting platforms are to modern development workflows and the importance of transparent incident communications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 现正发生宕机，核心服务性能受影响。此次故障凸显代码托管平台在现代开发工作流中的关键作用，以及对透明事故通告的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://user-images.githubusercontent.com/19292210/60553863-044dd200-9cea-11e9-987e-7db84449f215.png' data-full='https://user-images.githubusercontent.com/19292210/60553863-044dd200-9cea-11e9-987e-7db84449f215.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47999636' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47999636' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://partyon.xyz/@nullagent/116499715071759135' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BYOMesh – New LoRa mesh radio offers 100x the bandwidth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BYOMesh：新一代 LoRa 网状无线电带来 100 倍带宽</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 324</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47999636' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BYOMesh launches a LoRa mesh radio claiming 100x the bandwidth of typical LoRa devices, enabling more data-rich IoT, firmware updates, and real-time telemetry over long ranges. The move highlights how higher-throughput needs are pushing mesh/LPWAN technology beyond traditional use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BYOMesh 推出声称比常规 LoRa 设备快 100 倍带宽的网状无线电，可能支持更高数据量的物联网、固件更新和远程遥测等。此举反映了在大范围低功耗网络里，对更高吞吐的需求正在推动新应用场景的产生。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://partyon.xyz/packs/media/images/logo-d4b5dc90fd3e117d141ae7053b157f58.svg' data-full='https://partyon.xyz/packs/media/images/logo-d4b5dc90fd3e117d141ae7053b157f58.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47939320' data-tags='AI,Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47939320' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stratechery.com/2026/an-interview-with-openai-ceo-sam-altman-and-aws-ceo-matt-garman-about-bedrock-managed-agents/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI models coming to Amazon Bedrock: Interview with OpenAI and AWS CEOs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 模型将入驻 Amazon Bedrock：OpenAI 与 AWS 首席执行官访谈</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 320</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47939320' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bedrock will host OpenAI models, enabling AWS customers to run OpenAI capabilities within a managed AWS service. The interview sheds light on governance, privacy, and how enterprises might leverage this integration for scalable AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bedrock 将托管 OpenAI 模型，方便 AWS 用户在托管的 AWS 服务中使用 OpenAI 能力。访谈呈现了治理、隐私以及企业如何利用这一集成实现规模化 AI 部署的前景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvaTAud3AuY29tXC9zdHJhdGVjaGVyeS5jb21cL3dwLWNvbnRlbnRcL3VwbG9hZHNcLzIwMThcLzAzXC9jcm9wcGVkLWFuZHJvaWQtY2hyb21lLTUxMng1MTItMS5wbmc_Zml0PTUxMiUyQzUxMiZzc2w9MSIsInR4dCI6IlN0cmF0ZWNoZXJ5IGJ5IEJlbiBUaG9tcHNvbiIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxODgwNDM0MTV9.5VWck4PcKPWCTPe_HVznn3n3xsgn-G0b3d2OeiNNC7cMQ' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvaTAud3AuY29tXC9zdHJhdGVjaGVyeS5jb21cL3dwLWNvbnRlbnRcL3VwbG9hZHNcLzIwMThcLzAzXC9jcm9wcGVkLWFuZHJvaWQtY2hyb21lLTUxMng1MTItMS5wbmc_Zml0PTUxMiUyQzUxMiZzc2w9MSIsInR4dCI6IlN0cmF0ZWNoZXJ5IGJ5IEJlbiBUaG9tcHNvbiIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxODgwNDM0MTV9.5VWck4PcKPWCTPe_HVznn3n3xsgn-G0b3d2OeiNNC7cMQ' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48000152' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48000152' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/attributed-to-banksy-a-new-statue-of-a-suited-man-blinded-by-a-flag-and-walking-off-a-ledge-appeared-in-central-london-180988662/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New statue in London, attributed to Banksy, of a suited man, blinded by a flag</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伦敦出现新雕像 归属 Banksy 的西装男子被旗帜遮眼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48000152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 315</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48000152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 298</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48000152' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports a new Banksy-attributed statue in central London depicting a suited man blinded by a flag, continuing Banksy’s signature social commentary in public sculpture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道伦敦市中心出现一尊据称归属于 Banksy 的雕像，描绘一名西装男子被旗帜遮眼，延续 Banksy 的公共艺术社会讽刺。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/-E_L8LWmpxRR5Ckvs1TP3dR79rA=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(2500x1667:2501x1668)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/6a/4a/6a4a7ecb-3a7c-4ac9-9297-c4cce0ec91b3/new_statue.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/-E_L8LWmpxRR5Ckvs1TP3dR79rA=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(2500x1667:2501x1668)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/6a/4a/6a4a7ecb-3a7c-4ac9-9297-c4cce0ec91b3/new_statue.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47967289' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47967289' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/politics/policy/senators-vote-to-ban-themselves-from-trading-on-prediction-markets-ae4535dd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. Senators Vote to Ban Themselves from Trading on Prediction Markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国参议员投票禁止自己在预测市场交易</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47967289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47967289' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47967289' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a rare move, U.S. Senators voted to ban themselves from trading on prediction markets. The rule aims to curb conflicts of interest and insider-information concerns, though critics argue it may be inconsistent with broader market experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国参议员投票禁止自己在预测市场进行交易，旨在降低利益冲突和内幕信息风险。批评者则质疑此类自我约束是否与更广泛的市场试验规则相一致。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47936156' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47936156' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/919494/google-pentagon-classified-ai-deal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google and Pentagon reportedly agree on deal for &#x27;any lawful&#x27; use of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌与五角大楼就“任何合法用途”的 AI 达成协议（传闻）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47936156' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google and the Pentagon reportedly struck a deal allowing broad, &#x27;any lawful&#x27; uses of Google&#x27;s AI by the military. The arrangement underscores growing private-sector involvement in defense AI and raises questions about governance, safety, and civilian oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据传，谷歌与五角大楼就‘任何合法用途’的 AI 达成协议，允许军事部门在广泛范围内使用谷歌的 AI。此举反映出私营企业在国防 AI 中的参与度日益加深，同时引发关于治理、安全与民众监督的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/24925007/236780_Google_AntiTrust_Trial_Custom_Art_CVirginia__0001_5.png?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/chorus/uploads/chorus_asset/file/24925007/236780_Google_AntiTrust_Trial_Custom_Art_CVirginia__0001_5.png?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47988742' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47988742' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/clypjx3rg2go' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California to begin ticketing driverless cars that violate traffic laws</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州将对违反交通法规的无人驾驶汽车开罚单</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 304</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 320</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47988742' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California plans to ticket autonomous cars that violate traffic laws through automated enforcement, pushing accountability onto AV operators. The move accelerates safety governance for on-road robo-taxis and tests how regulators handle software-driven infractions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州计划对违反交通法规的无人驾驶汽车进行自动执法并开罚单，将问责落在运营方身上。此举加速了对路上机器人出租车的安全治理，并测试监管机构对软件性违规的处理方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/4bd8/live/9ece7410-4634-11f1-b14f-8905f097c360.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/4bd8/live/9ece7410-4634-11f1-b14f-8905f097c360.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47949204' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source,Health,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47949204' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/GliaX/Stethoscope' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An open-source stethoscope that costs between $2.5 and $5 to produce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成本仅2.5到5美元的开源听诊器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949204' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949204' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47949204' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source stethoscope project can be produced for roughly $2.5–$5, illustrating how affordable hardware can empower medical access and education. It demonstrates how community-driven hardware can compress costs without compromising basic functionality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个开源听诊器项目的单位生产成本约为2.5–5美元，展示了低成本硬件如何推动医疗普及与教育。此类社区驱动的硬件能在不牺牲基本功能的前提下降低门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/063c0f212099fc01ed837ade038c610c95b8c5c7848d7cfaa00f0dba3655136d/GliaX/Stethoscope' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/063c0f212099fc01ed837ade038c610c95b8c5c7848d7cfaa00f0dba3655136d/GliaX/Stethoscope' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47922079' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47922079' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lawrencecpaulson.github.io//2026/04/23/Why_not_Lean.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Why not just use Lean?”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何不直接用 Lean？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47922079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47922079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47922079' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lean isn&#x27;t a universal cure; the post argues for applying Lean where it fits and avoiding dogmatic adoption. It stresses context, trade-offs, and disciplined implementation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lean 并非万能药，文中主张在合适场景下应用 Lean，避免教条化的采用。文章强调情境、取舍与落地执行的平衡，以及对团队的长期影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47961378' data-tags='Data,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47961378' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bidprowl.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I aggregated 28 US Government auction sites into one search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把 28 个美国政府拍卖网站聚合成一个搜索入口</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47961378' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I aggregated 28 US government auction sites into one search tool to simplify finding assets, surplus equipment, and procurement opportunities. The project illustrates the value of unifying public-data sources to boost efficiency, while data freshness and reliability remain important.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者把 28 个美国政府拍卖网站聚合为一个检索入口，便于发现资产、闲置设备与采购机会。此举体现整合公开数据源以提升效率的趋势，但数据的时效性与可信度同样重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bidprowl.com/api/og?title=Government+Surplus+Auctions+in+One+Place&amp;subtitle=Search+GSA,+GovDeals,+GovPlanet+and+more' data-full='https://bidprowl.com/api/og?title=Government+Surplus+Auctions+in+One+Place&amp;subtitle=Search+GSA,+GovDeals,+GovPlanet+and+more' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47960507' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47960507' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://firethering.com/granite-4-1-ibm-open-source-model-family/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Granite 4.1: IBM&#x27;s 8B Model Matching 32B MoE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Granite 4.1：IBM 的 8B 模型与 32B MoE</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47960507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47960507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47960507' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Granite 4.1 introduces open-source tooling for aligning an 8B base model with a 32B MoE (Mixture of Experts) architecture, enabling scalable inference and modular model design. It signals IBM&#x27;s ongoing push to empower open AI tooling and explore mixture-of-experts approaches for efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Granite 4.1 是一个开源模型家族，展示将一个 8B 基模型与 32B MoE 架构结合的能力，支持可扩展推理与模块化模型设计。体现 IBM 在开源 AI 工具与专家门控混合模型领域的持续探索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://firethering.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Granite-4.1-IBMs-8B-Model-Is-Competing-With-Models-Four-Times-Its-Size.jpg' data-full='https://firethering.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Granite-4.1-IBMs-8B-Model-Is-Competing-With-Models-Four-Times-Its-Size.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48000028' data-tags='Design,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48000028' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wiki.alcidesfonseca.com/blog/why-tuis-are-back/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why TUIs are back</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TUI（可触知用户界面）为何回归</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48000028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48000028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 313</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48000028' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tangible user interfaces are making a comeback as advances in fabrication, sensing, and materials enable new physical-digital interactions. The trend favors embodied learning, collaboration, and accessible design through rapid prototyping.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>可触知用户界面正重新兴起，得益于制材、传感与材料领域的进步，使物理数字互动更直观。该潮流推动以身临其境的学习、协作和可及性为目标的快速原型设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wiki.alcidesfonseca.com/static/images/alcides_small.jpg' data-full='https://wiki.alcidesfonseca.com/static/images/alcides_small.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47961004' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47961004' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gcc.gnu.org/gcc-16/changes.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GCC 16 has been released</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GCC 16 已发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47961004' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Version 16 of GCC ships with improved diagnostics, broader C/C++ standard support, and performance improvements across platforms. It also introduces new options and stricter defaults that may affect existing build configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GCC 16 提供改进的诊断、扩展的 C/C++ 标准支持以及跨平台的性能改进。新选项与更严格的默认设置可能影响现有的编译配置。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47938097' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47938097' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.claude.com/incidents/9l93x2ht4s5w' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude.ai unavailable and elevated errors on the API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude.ai 不可用及 API 出错上升</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47938097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47938097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47938097' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude AI&#x27;s API experiences outages and elevated errors, impacting developers relying on its services. The incident highlights the fragility of AI-as-a-service platforms and the importance of resilience and multi-provider strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude AI 的 API 遭遇宕机与高错误率，影响依赖该服务的开发者。此事件凸显 AI 即服务平台的脆弱性，以及提高弹性和采用多供应商策略的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/assets/logos/favicon-2b86ed00cfa6258307d4a3d0c482fd733c7973f82de213143b24fc062c540367.png' data-full='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/assets/logos/favicon-2b86ed00cfa6258307d4a3d0c482fd733c7973f82de213143b24fc062c540367.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/changelog/2026-04-27-github-copilot-code-review-will-start-consuming-github-actions-minutes-on-june-1-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub Copilot code review will start consuming GitHub Actions minutes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub Copilot 代码审查将开始消耗 GitHub Actions 的使用分钟数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47932028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 294</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47932028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47932028' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Copilot Code Review will start consuming GitHub Actions minutes from June 1, affecting code-review workloads and project costs. Teams will need to optimize usage or budget for the additional minutes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Copilot 代码审查将自6月1日起开始消耗 GitHub Actions 的分钟数，影响代码审查工作流和项目成本。团队需要优化使用量，或为额外的分钟数调整预算。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://larsfaye.com/articles/agentic-coding-is-a-trap' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agentic Coding Is a Trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理编码是一个陷阱</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002442' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that agency-focused coding approaches, where AI agents take control, risk brittle behavior and hidden dependencies; recommends robust design, monitoring, and fallback strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出让 AI 代理参与编码的做法可能导致易崩溃的行为与隐藏的依赖，建议采用坚固的设计、监控与回退策略。</p>

The takeaway is a practical blueprint for engineers evaluating or contributing to similar tooling.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260428-ai-companies-want-you-to-be-afraid-of-them' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why AI companies want you to be afraid of them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 AI 公司要让你害怕它们</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47949750' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BBC Future argues that AI firms frequently frame risks as existential threats to push regulation and funding, shaping public perception and policy. While there are real concerns around misinformation, bias, and labor disruption, the piece urges a nuanced view of risk that weighs innovation against safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BBC 指出 AI 公司常以风险即生存威胁来推动监管与投资，影响公众与政策走向。确实存在错误信息、偏见和劳动冲击等真实风险，但文章倡导在鼓励创新的同时谨慎对待风险与治理。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47976856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47976856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 300</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47976856' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spotify adds Verified badges to differentiate human artists from AI-generated content, signaling a move toward transparency in streaming metadata and potential new monetization rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Spotify 推出 Verified 徽章以区分真人艺人与 AI 生成内容，表明在流媒体元数据透明度方面向前迈出一步，并可能引入新的货币化规则。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47990284' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.universiteitleiden.nl/en/news/2025/07/neanderthals-ran-fat-factories-125000-years-ago' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neanderthals ran &#x27;fat factories&#x27; 125k years ago (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>125,000 年前尼安德特人运营脂肪工厂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47990284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47990284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47990284' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New archaeological findings suggest Neanderthals ran large-scale fat-processing activities 125,000 years ago, hinting at sophisticated subsistence strategies and social organization. The discovery adds nuance to our understanding of Neanderthal cognition and energy management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新发现表明 125,000 年前的尼安德特人进行大规模脂肪加工，暴露出复杂的生计策略与社群组织能力，丰富了对其认知与能量管理的理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.universiteitleiden.nl/binaries/content/gallery/ul2/main-images/archaeology/neumark-banner.jpg/neumark-banner.jpg/d600x315' data-full='https://www.universiteitleiden.nl/binaries/content/gallery/ul2/main-images/archaeology/neumark-banner.jpg/neumark-banner.jpg/d600x315' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47940789' data-tags='Gaming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47940789' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/pc-gaming/denuvo-has-been-bypassed-in-all-single-player-games-it-previously-protected-2k-games-and-denuvo-reportedly-retaliate-with-mandatory-14-day-online-checks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Denuvo has been cracked in all single-player games it previously protected</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Denuvo 已被破解，覆盖同类单人游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47940789' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Denuvo’s anti-tamper protections have been cracked across all single-player titles it previously protected, signaling a continuing piracy vulnerability in boxed and digitally distributed PC games. Publishers may respond with countermeasures and new DRM strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Denuvo 的防篡改保护已被破解，波及此前所保护的所有单人游戏，显示 PC 游戏的盗版防护依然脆弱。发行方可能采纳新的对策与 DRM 策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/NgNprVVnWz46KEcoC6U6V8-2048-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/NgNprVVnWz46KEcoC6U6V8-2048-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47917026' data-tags='Programming,Web,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47917026' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.chrome.com/docs/ai/prompt-api' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Prompt API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>提示 API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47917026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47917026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47917026' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API defines a standardized way for apps to collect and manage prompts for AI features, aiming to simplify integration and improve user privacy controls. The post outlines typical usage patterns and what this means for developers building AI assistants in the browser. It signals a shift toward more uniform AI integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chrome 的提示 API 为应用收集和管理 AI 提示提供了标准化接口，简化集成并强化用户隐私控制。文中概述了常见的使用模式，以及这对在浏览器中构建 AI 助手的开发者意味着什么，标志着 AI 集成趋于统一。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer.chrome.com/static/docs/ai/prompt-api/images/cover.png' data-full='https://developer.chrome.com/static/docs/ai/prompt-api/images/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
