<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47922957' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://muffin.ink/blog/scratch-svg-sanitization/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The woes of sanitizing SVGs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>清理 SVG 的烦恼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47922957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47922957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47922957' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sanitizing SVGs remains tricky due to vector graphics features, embedded scripts, and CSS, leading to security and performance pitfalls. The post outlines pitfalls and best practices for safer SVG handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 SVG 进行安全清洗仍然颇具挑战，原因在于矢量特性、嵌入式脚本与 CSS 的组合带来的安全和性能风险。文中概述了难点与更安全的处理实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://muffin.ink/blog/scratch-svg-sanitization/blue.png' data-full='https://muffin.ink/blog/scratch-svg-sanitization/blue.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47976529' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ij.org/police-have-reportedly-used-license-plate-readers-to-stalk-romantic-interests-at-least-14-times-in-recent-years/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Police Have Used License Plate Readers at Least 14x to Stalk Romantic Interests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警方至少14次使用车牌识别系统监视恋爱对象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47976529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47976529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47976529' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>At least 14 documented instances where police used license plate readers to stalk romantic interests. The report highlights civil-liberties risks and underscores the need for guardrails on surveillance tech, including access controls, data retention, and purpose limitation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，警察至少在14起案件中利用车牌识别系统来监视恋爱对象。这暴露出监控技术在个人私隐领域的滥用风险，呼吁对数据用途、保留期限和访问权限设定更严格的规范。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47940271' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47940271' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/04/no-fly-zones-around-moving-ice-vehicles-this-drone-pilot-fought-back-and-won/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Drone pilot makes US rescind no-fly zones around unmarked, moving ICE vehicles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无人机飞手推动美国撤销围绕移动中的未标记 ICE 车辆的禁飞区</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 263</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47940271' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A drone pilot challenged no-fly zones around moving, unmarked ICE vehicles, resulting in the zones being rescinded. The case highlights the tension between aviation regulation and real-world drone operations, potentially influencing how authorities manage dynamic airspace.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名无人机飞行员挑战了围绕未标记、正在移动的 ICE 车辆的禁飞区，最终禁飞区被撤销。此事凸显在动态场景下的空域管控与无人机执行之间的矛盾，或将影响未来的相关政策与执法方式。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47923010' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eclecticlight.co/2026/04/23/networking-changes-coming-in-macos-27/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Networking changes coming in macOS 27</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 27 的网络改动将带来哪些变化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47923010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 263</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47923010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47923010' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple plans network stack rework in macOS 27; changes include new APIs, routing behavior, and privacy controls that may affect developers&#x27; apps, VPNs, and network extensions. The changes signal Apple&#x27;s broader push to tighten network security and streamline the stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果在 macOS 27 中重构网络栈，涉及新 API、路由行为及隐私控制等更新，可能影响应用、VPN 与网络扩展的实现。此举体现了苹果在网络安全与栈简化方面的持续推进。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47950022' data-tags='Politics,Legal,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47950022' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.chrisbrunet.com/p/third-editor-fired-in-elseviers-citation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Third editor fired in Elsevier’s citation cartel crackdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Elsevier 引用卡特尔行动中第三名编辑被解雇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47950022' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The ongoing crackdown on manipulated citations inside Elsevier has claimed another editor, marking the third dismissal in the effort. The moves underscore publishers&#x27; effort to restore integrity in peer review amidst concerns about citation pressure and journal metrics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Elsevier 的引用操控打击行动中，另一名编辑被解雇，成为此轮行动的第三位被解雇者。此次处置凸显学术出版界为提升同行评审与期刊指标的诚信所作出的持续努力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!nRg1!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fffd043cd-49b8-4264-b0d3-8032fc23ca39_1911x1070.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!nRg1!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fffd043cd-49b8-4264-b0d3-8032fc23ca39_1911x1070.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47945707' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47945707' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jointhefreeworld.org/blog/articles/lisps/why-i-still-reach-for-scheme-instead-of-haskell/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I still reach for Lisp and Scheme instead of Haskell</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何我仍偏爱 Lisp 和 Scheme 而不是 Haskell</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47945707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47945707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 148</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47945707' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues Lisp and Scheme offer macro systems, minimal syntax, and REPL-driven development that some programmers prefer over Haskell&#x27;s strong type system. It advocates returning to the elegance of Lisp-family languages for exploration and rapid iteration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张 Lisp 与 Scheme 的宏系统、简洁语法和交互式开发仍具魅力，优于 Haskell 的类型驱动风格。倡导在探索与快速迭代中回归 Lisp 系语言的简洁与灵活。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47984852' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47984852' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eclecticlight.co/2026/05/02/how-fast-is-a-macos-vm-and-how-small-could-it-be/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How fast is a macOS VM, and how small could it be?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 虚拟机有多快，能有多小？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47984852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47984852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47984852' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article benchmarks macOS VM performance on Apple hardware and explores how small a viable macOS VM can be. It discusses CPU, memory, and disk I/O tradeoffs and what they mean for dev/test workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章测评了在苹果硬件上运行的 macOS 虚拟机性能，并探讨了可用的最小化体积与实际可用性之间的权衡。内容聚焦 CPU、内存和磁盘 I/O 的取舍，以及对开发/测试工作流的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://eclecticlight.co/wp-content/uploads/2015/01/cropped-eclecticlightlogo-e1421784280911.png?w=200' data-full='https://eclecticlight.co/wp-content/uploads/2015/01/cropped-eclecticlightlogo-e1421784280911.png?w=200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.blender.org/press/anthropic-joins-the-blender-development-fund-as-corporate-patron/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic Joins the Blender Development Fund as Corporate Patron</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 作为企业赞助商加入 Blender 开发基金会</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47936370' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic has joined the Blender Development Fund as a corporate patron, backing the open-source 3D software Blender. The move signals growing corporate support for open-source tooling used in AI, simulations, and content pipelines, and could accelerate Blender&#x27;s development while aligning with responsible AI principles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 已成为 Blender 开发基金会的企业赞助商，支持开源的 3D 创作套件 Blender。此举表明企业对开源工具的日益支持，尤其是在 AI、仿真和内容创作工作流中的应用，可能推动 Blender 的发展并与负责任 AI 原则保持一致。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.blender.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/anthropic_dev_fund-480x270.png' data-full='https://www.blender.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/anthropic_dev_fund-480x270.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The piece distills actionable tips for developers seeking faster history operations and smoother collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ted Nyman 深入探討 Git 的高性能實作，分享針對大型倉庫與複雜工作流程的基準與實用優化策略。內容提煉出可直接落地的做法，幫助開發者提升歷史操作的速度與協作流暢度。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Domestic surveillance in the United States is expanding through a growing mix of government programs and private data partnerships, broadening monitoring reach into everyday life. The piece weighs safety gains against privacy trade-offs, and notes mounting concern from lawmakers and civil-liberties advocates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国国内监控通过政府计划与私营数据伙伴关系的日益扩张，正在拓展对日常生活的监控覆盖。文章在安全收益与隐私代价之间权衡，并指出立法者和公民自由倡导者的日益关切。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FDA 批准首个用于治疗遗传性听力丧失的基因治疗</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47919733' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FDA approves the first gene therapy for a genetic form of hearing loss, marking a milestone for genetic medicine. The therapy demonstrates how targeted gene interventions can restore function and may accelerate approvals and reimbursement discussions for similar approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FDA 批准首个用于治疗遗传性听力损失的基因治疗，标志着基因医学的重要里程碑。该治疗展示了定向基因干预在恢复听力方面的潜力，并可能推动类似疗法的快速获批与报销讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47942492' data-tags='Security,AI'>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47942492' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/49363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Regression: malware reminder on every read still causes subagent refusals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回归：每次读取时的恶意软件提醒仍导致子代理拒绝</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47942492' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47942492' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47942492' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A regression in AI tooling causes malware-reminder prompts to fire on every read, triggering subagents to refuse tasks. The behavior highlights the tension between aggressive safety prompts and workflow usability. It suggests tighter prompt control and more robust delegation logic to avoid blocking legitimate operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 工具链中出现回归：每次读取都会触发恶意软件提醒，导致子代理拒绝执行任务。这体现出安全提醒的强硬风格与工作流可用性之间的张力。文章建议更精确的提示控制和更健壮的任务分发逻辑，以避免阻塞正常操作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1064771c48ee8924f01657569f38b44040f2a114639cd110cdf58bf25a4cd0db/anthropics/claude-code/issues/49363' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1064771c48ee8924f01657569f38b44040f2a114639cd110cdf58bf25a4cd0db/anthropics/claude-code/issues/49363' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47998790' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thegamer.com/mgs2-hd-edition-source-code-massive-leak/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Metal Gear Solid 2&#x27;s source code has been leaked on 4chan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>4chan 上 Metal Gear Solid 2 源代码泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998790' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47998790' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The leak reportedly exposes Metal Gear Solid 2&#x27;s source code, fueling discussion about preservation, modding, and IP risk for classic games. It spotlights how legacy code can reappear and affect fan projects and legal considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称泄露的是《合金装备 2》的源代码，引发关于游戏保留、模组开发以及版权风险的讨论。凸显了遗留代码可能再次曝光对粉丝项目和法律的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.thegamerimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/new-project-65.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.thegamerimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/new-project-65.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47912645' data-tags='Politics,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47912645' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://road.cc/news/driverless-taxis-veering-into-cycle-lanes-normal-practice-says-waymo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Waymo says can&#x27;t avoid bike lanes because riders want to be dropped off in them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Waymo称无法避免自行车道，因为乘客希望在自行车道上进行下车</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47912645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 245</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47912645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 425</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47912645' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Waymo argues its autonomous taxis cannot always avoid bike lanes because riders prefer curbside drop-offs in those lanes. This raises safety and regulatory questions about autonomous driving in urban bike infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Waymo声称难以完全避开自行车道，因为乘客希望在自行车道内下车。这暴露出自动驾驶在城市自行车道中的安全与监管挑战，并引发对路线选择与风险的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.road.cc/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Waymo-London.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.road.cc/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Waymo-London.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47994012' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47994012' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://acai.sh/blog/specsmaxxing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Specsmaxxing – On overcoming AI psychosis, and why I write specs in YAML</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Specsmaxxing：克服 AI 的认知失衡，以及为何用 YAML 编写规格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47994012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 243</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47994012' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 254</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47994012' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Specmaxxing argues that AI hype can be tempered by disciplined, machine-readable specs. It advocates using YAML to encode requirements, tests, and acceptance criteria to keep expectations realistic. The piece ties rigorous specification to clearer human–AI collaboration and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Specsmaxxing 指出，通过严格、机器可读的规格可以抑制 AI 的过度炒作。主张用 YAML 将需求、测试和验收标准编码，以保持期望现实且便于沟通。将规范化写作视为改进人机协作和治理的关键手段。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://acai.mintlify.app/mintlify-assets/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DDocumentation%26title%3DSpecsmaxxing%26description%3DOn%2Bovercoming%2BAI%2Bpsychosis%252C%2Band%2Bwhy%2BI%2Bwrite%2Bspecs%2Bin%2BYAML%2B%2528plus%2Bopen-sourcing%2Ba%2Btoolkit%2Bfor%2Byou%2Bto%2Btry%2529%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Facai%252F64tMg2VKm-ey5cuT%252Flogo-word.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253D64tMg2VKm-ey5cuT%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D95b3095af445779b3cd61dc26931fa84%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Facai%252F64tMg2VKm-ey5cuT%252Flogo-word.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253D64tMg2VKm-ey5cuT%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D95b3095af445779b3cd61dc26931fa84%26primaryColor%3D%2523930d7a%26lightColor%3D%2523f765f7%26darkColor%3D%25231a0416%26backgroundLight%3D%2523fcf8fb%26backgroundDark%3D%2523140411&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' data-full='https://acai.mintlify.app/mintlify-assets/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DDocumentation%26title%3DSpecsmaxxing%26description%3DOn%2Bovercoming%2BAI%2Bpsychosis%252C%2Band%2Bwhy%2BI%2Bwrite%2Bspecs%2Bin%2BYAML%2B%2528plus%2Bopen-sourcing%2Ba%2Btoolkit%2Bfor%2Byou%2Bto%2Btry%2529%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Facai%252F64tMg2VKm-ey5cuT%252Flogo-word.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253D64tMg2VKm-ey5cuT%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D95b3095af445779b3cd61dc26931fa84%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Facai%252F64tMg2VKm-ey5cuT%252Flogo-word.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253D64tMg2VKm-ey5cuT%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D95b3095af445779b3cd61dc26931fa84%26primaryColor%3D%2523930d7a%26lightColor%3D%2523f765f7%26darkColor%3D%25231a0416%26backgroundLight%3D%2523fcf8fb%26backgroundDark%3D%2523140411&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47991350' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/05/02/this-tesla-owner-won-10k-in-court-for-teslas-fsd-lies-tesla-is-still-fighting-him/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla owner won $10k in court for Tesla&#x27;s FSD lies. Tesla is still fighting him</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉车主因FSD宣传获1万美元赔偿，特斯拉仍在上诉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47991350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 243</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47991350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47991350' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Tesla owner won a $10,000 verdict over FSD-related misrepresentations, highlighting how marketing claims for driver-assistance features can expose manufacturers to liability. Tesla is appealing, signaling ongoing legal scrutiny of autopilot-style promises and their real-world implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名特斯拉车主就FSD相关宣传获得一万美元赔偿，凸显厂商在驾驶辅助功能宣传中的潜在法律责任。特斯拉正在上诉，意味着对自动驾驶相关承诺的持续法律审查及其在实际使用中的影响。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47933257' data-tags='Security,DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47933257' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nesbitt.io/2026/04/28/github-actions-is-the-weakest-link.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub Actions is the weakest link</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub Actions 是现代软件管道中的薄弱环节</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47933257' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical examination argues GitHub Actions is the systemic weak link in modern software pipelines, highlighting security and governance risks of bundling CI/CD with code hosting. The piece calls for stronger isolation and supply-chain safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 GitHub Actions 的批判性审视认为它是现代软件管道的系统性薄弱环节，强调将 CI/CD 与代码托管捆绑在一起所带来的安全与治理风险。文章呼吁加强隔离与供应链防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nesbitt.io/images/boxes.png' data-full='https://nesbitt.io/images/boxes.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47947490' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47947490' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.diabettech.com/i-asked-ai-to-count-my-carbs-27000-times-it-couldnt-give-me-the-same-answer-twice/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>He asked AI to count carbs 27000 times. It couldn&#x27;t give the same answer twice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>他让 AI 连续统计碳水化合物 27000 次，结果不能给出同样的答案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47947490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47947490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47947490' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical experiment reveals that large language models struggle to produce consistent answers when asked the same counting task thousands of times. Reproducibility remains a challenge due to prompt, randomness, and model state, underscoring the need for deterministic modes or controlled prompts in critical tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过让 AI 连续回答同一碳水化合物统计问题数万次，作者发现输出无法始终如一，反映了大语言模型在可重复性方面的局限。结果强调在关键任务中需要确定性设置或更严格的提示控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/www.diabettech.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/diabettech_fig_insulin_risk.png?resize=678%2C381&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/www.diabettech.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/diabettech_fig_insulin_risk.png?resize=678%2C381&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47910546' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/orgs/community/discussions/192666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Issue links now open in a popup</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>问题链接现以弹出式窗口打开</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47910546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47910546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47910546' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A UX change brings issue links into a popup dialogue, changing how developers reference related discussions. It can speed scanning but may disrupt multi-tasking and raise accessibility concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的用户体验将议题链接放入弹出对话框，改变了开发者引用相关讨论的方式。此举提高了快速浏览，但可能打断多任务工作流并引发无障碍方面的问题。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phulin.me/blog/simtower' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse Engineering SimTower</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向工程 SimTower</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47937814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47937814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47937814' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical post explores reverse engineering the classic SimTower, revealing how its simulation logic and scheduling work under the hood. The write-up serves as a case study in analyzing legacy sim software and could inspire methods for modern game/tool reverse engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>技术文章揭秘对经典游戏 SimTower 的逆向工程，揭示其内部仿真逻辑与调度机制。作为对遗留仿真软件分析的案例，或为现代游戏与工具的逆向研究提供思路。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47914677' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/butterflies-are-in-dramatic-decline-across-north-america-a-close-look-at-the-western-monarch-shows-why-180988582/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Butterflies are in decline across North America, a look at the Western Monarch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>北美蝴蝶数量下降：以西部君蝶为例</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 238</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47914677' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Butterflies are in decline across North America, with Western Monarchs emblematic of broader pollinator losses. Habitat loss, pesticide exposure, and climate shifts are central pressures, signaling ecosystem-wide consequences that extend beyond butterflies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>北美蝴蝶数量持续下降，西部君蝶成为更广泛传粉者损失的缩影。栖息地消失、农药暴露和气候变化是主要压力，预示着对生态系统的广泛影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/-pUy-tpdhYEeFuT3-STWXN5xIIc=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(2784x1856:2785x1857)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/f0/cc/f0cccc48-0697-4243-89b0-1e93aa195dc2/dsc_0152.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/-pUy-tpdhYEeFuT3-STWXN5xIIc=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(2784x1856:2785x1857)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/f0/cc/f0cccc48-0697-4243-89b0-1e93aa195dc2/dsc_0152.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47960015' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.epo.org/en/news-events/news/inventions-battery-reuse-and-recycling-increase-more-seven-fold-last-decade' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inventions for battery reuse and recycling increase seven-fold in last decade</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>过去十年电池再利用与回收发明增长七倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47960015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 237</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47960015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47960015' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Patent filings for battery reuse and recycling have surged seven-fold over the last decade, signaling a strong shift toward circular economics in energy storage. The trend could influence policy, supply chains, and the pace of new battery tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>过去十年关于电池再利用与回收的专利申请暴增七倍，显示向能源存储的循环经济转型正在加速，可能推动相关政策、供应链与新型电池技术的进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.epo.org/themes/custom/epo_org/logo.svg' data-full='https://www.epo.org/themes/custom/epo_org/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47979839' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47979839' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://metin.nextc.org/posts/Credit_Cards_Are_Vulnerable_To_Brute_Force_Kind_Attacks.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Credit cards are vulnerable to brute force kind attacks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>信用卡易受暴力破解攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47979839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47979839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47979839' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security analysis shows that some card-processing setups can be exploited via offline brute-forcing of card numbers, enabling attackers to test large candidate pools until a valid number is found. The finding underscores the need for strong rate limiting, tokenization, and robust verification to prevent fraud in both card-present and online contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全分析显示，某些信用卡处理流程在离线环境下可能被暴力穷举卡号，攻击者可测试大量候选号码直至找到有效卡。这一发现凸显对速率限制、令牌化和强健验证的需求，以防止实体和在线交易中的欺诈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://metin.nextc.org/static/5/receipt.jpg' data-full='https://metin.nextc.org/static/5/receipt.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/FeSens/auto-arch-tournament/blob/main/docs/auto-arch-tournament-blog-post.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Auto-Architecture: Karpathy&#x27;s Loop, pointed at a CPU</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47937380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 233</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47937380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47937380' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN presenting Auto-Architecture, a loop-based system inspired by Karpathy that automatically designs and evaluates neural network architectures on commodity CPU hardware. It demonstrates end-to-end automation of architecture search without GPU dependence. The project highlights the growing appeal of accessible, automated ML design workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示 Auto-Architecture，这是一套受 Karpathy 思路启发、在普通 CPU 硬件上自动设计与评估神经网络架构的循环系统。它演示了无需 GPU 的端到端架构自动化搜索。该项目凸显了可负担性与自动化 ML 设计流程的日益吸引力。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wheresyoured.at/ais-economics-dont-make-sense/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI&#x27;s economics don&#x27;t make sense</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的经济学并不合理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47936867' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take argues that AI&#x27;s economics — from training costs to deployment pricing — don&#x27;t add up for sustainable business models. It calls for efficiency, model reuse, and new monetization strategies to align incentives across developers, platforms, and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇观点文章认为 AI 的经济学在成本结构、定价等方面并不具备可持续性，呼吁提升效率、重复使用模型并探索新的盈利模式，以实现开发者、平台与用户之间的激励对齐。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/05/01/roblox-rblx-stock-child-safety-earnings.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Roblox shares plummet 18% as child safety measures weigh on bookings</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47988261' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Roblox&#x27;s stock fell after earnings as safety-focused changes dampened bookings, signaling a tension between platform safety and monetization. The result underscores how child-safety measures and parental controls can materially impact growth in consumer-facing online services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Roblox 在财报后股价下跌约 18%，因为以安全为导向的变动压缩了订单量。这凸显了平台安全与商业化之间的矛盾，儿童安全措施和家长控制对面向消费者的在线服务增长有实际影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着企业加快数字化转型与人工智能落地，对软件工程师的需求持续上升，招聘数量增加，行业竞争加剧。这一趋势往往伴随更高薪资与对实际能力的更严格评估。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47936210' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FCC funding notes indicate Paramount would be 49.5% foreign-owned after the merger, raising questions about foreign ownership thresholds and regulatory oversight. The data could influence approvals and conditions around cross-border media investments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FCC 的资金申请显示，合并后 Paramount 的外国所有权将达到 49.5%，引发对外国所有权门槛与监管的讨论。这一数字可能影响跨境媒体投资的审批与相关条件。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47963316' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://honker.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Honker – Durable queues, streams, pub/sub, and cron scheduler in a SQLite file</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SQLite 文件中的持久化队列、流、Pub/Sub 与 cron 调度器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47963316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47963316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47963316' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A single SQLite file can host durable queues, streams, pub/sub, and a cron-like scheduler, enabling complex data workflows without external services. This approach can be appealing for edge apps or offline-first systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 SQLite 文件就能承载持久化队列、流、发布/订阅和类似 cron 的调度功能，从而在不依赖外部服务的情况下实现复杂数据工作流。文中探讨如何在一个紧凑的数据存储中实现原语，权衡简单性与潜在的性能、可移植性之间的取舍，适合边缘计算或离线优先场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://honker.dev/og-image.png' data-full='https://honker.dev/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47946899' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tcd.ie/news_events/articles/2026/caedmons-hymn-discovery/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New copy of earliest poem in English, written 1,3k years ago, discovered in Rome</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在罗马发现的最早英语诗新抄本（距今约1300年）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47946899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47946899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47946899' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A newly discovered copy of Caedmon’s Hymn—one of the oldest known English poems—has been found in Rome. The find expands the manuscript trail of early Old English verse and could yield new insights into linguistic features and performance contexts from the era. It also underscores how medieval texts circulated far beyond their likely origin.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在罗马发现的《凯德蒙颂》最早英语诗歌新抄本被揭示。此发现扩展了早期古英语诗歌的手稿传承，为语言特征与表演传统提供新线索，并显示中世纪文本可能的跨地流传。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.tcd.ie/news_events/articles/2026/caedmons-hymn-discovery/media/tcd/news-images/Elisabetta-Magnanti-and-Mark-Faulkner-with-the-Trinity-copy-of-Bedes-Ecclesiastical-History.-To-be-clear-this-is-not-the-manuscript-they-found-in-Rome---.JPG' data-full='https://www.tcd.ie/news_events/articles/2026/caedmons-hymn-discovery/media/tcd/news-images/Elisabetta-Magnanti-and-Mark-Faulkner-with-the-Trinity-copy-of-Bedes-Ecclesiastical-History.-To-be-clear-this-is-not-the-manuscript-they-found-in-Rome---.JPG' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47985750' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes using coding agents as design engines to automate design tasks, enabling code-driven workflows for UI prototyping. This approach demonstrates AI-assisted design at scale and the potential for tighter human-AI collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出把编码代理用作设计引擎，自动化设计任务并推动 UI 原型的代码驱动工作流。这种方式展现了大规模的 AI 辅助设计潜力，以及人机协作的新可能。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47992349' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/05/01/business/surveillance-pricing-groceries-maryland.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Maryland to ban A.I.-driven price increases in grocery stores</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马里兰成为首个禁止 AI 驱动涨价的超市定价的州</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47992349' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Maryland passes a first-in-the-nation ban on AI-driven price increases in grocery stores, aiming to curb personalized pricing powered by consumer data. The move raises policy questions about fairness, transparency, and consumer protection in data-driven pricing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马里兰州成为首个禁止超市使用 AI 驱动定价策略的州，意在限制基于消费者数据的个性化定价。此举引发关于公平性、透明度与消费者保护之间的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://midnightmurmurations.substack.com/p/the-x-files-has-made-me-nostalgic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The X-Files has made me nostalgic for a time I never experienced</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《X档案》让我怀念一段我从未经历过的时光</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 203</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977583' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reflects on how The X-Files stirs nostalgia for a tech-era people never experienced, shaping attitudes toward mystery, data, and surveillance today. It uses fan nostalgia to comment on media influence and the evolution of tech culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者通过《X档案》唤起对未曾经历过的上世纪科技与社会氛围的怀念，进而反思当代对数据、监控和隐私的认知。文章借助流行文化的怀旧感，探讨媒体如何塑造我们对技术时代的想象。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20150417-00/?p=44213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are there both TMP and TEMP environment variables? (2015)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么同时有 TMP 与 TEMP 环境变量？（2015）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47984522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47984522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47984522' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains that TMP and TEMP designate temp directories; the variables reflect historical design choices and precedence rules. The note helps clarify why some software behaves differently and how to set them to avoid confusion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释 TMP 和 TEMP 都指向临时目录，这两个变量的存在源于历史设计与优先级规则。文中帮助读者理解为何有的软件表现不同，以及如何设置以避免混淆。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Biology is a Burrito uses a playful, visual/textual journey to explain the cell, making biology approachable for newcomers. It blends diagrams and narrative to demystify the unit of life and how cells function.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章以幽默的比喻和生动的图文，带读者从文本与图像双线索理解细胞，降低了生物学的门槛。通过插画与叙事，阐释生命单位细胞的结构与功能。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47927070' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47927070' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tps.ca/media-centre/stories/unprecedented-sms-blaster-arrests/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three men are facing charges in Toronto SMS Blaster arrests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多伦多短信轰炸案：三人被控44项罪名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47927070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47927070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47927070' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three men in Toronto have been charged on 44 counts tied to an SMS blaster operation, illustrating sustained crackdown on bulk texting abuse. The case spotlights enforcement tightening around illegal marketing practices and the tech, telecom, and compliance risks for companies relying on bulk messaging. It also shows prosecutors pursuing cross-border digital crimes as part of telecom fraud enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多伦多三名男子就一桩大规模短信轰炸案被控44项罪名，显示对垃圾短信的打击在加码。此案凸显对非法营销行为的执法加强，以及涉及企业在短信传播中的合规与风险。检方也在跨境数字犯罪打击中持续行动。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47902269' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47902269' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fabiensanglard.net/curse/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mo RAM, Mo Problems (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>內存之痛再升級（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47902269' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47902269' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Memory constraints continue to shape software design in 2025, pushing developers toward memory-aware data structures, smarter caching, and leaner runtimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年的軟件設計仍被內存限制左右，推動開發者採用更節省內存的資料結構、智慧快取與更輕量的執行環境。文章突顯內存使用與性能之間的實際取捨，對後端與嵌入式開發者具有參考價值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47957627' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47957627' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cauchy221/Alignment-Whack-a-Mole-Code' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alignment whack-a-mole: Finetuning activates recall of copyrighted books in LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对齐打地鼠：微调会让 LLM 记忆受版权保护书籍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47957627' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A &#x27;alignment whack-a-mole&#x27; project shows finetuning can cause LLMs to recall copyrighted book passages, highlighting risks of data memorization in trained models. The work prompts discussion on safe alignment techniques and copyright compliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个名为对齐打地鼠的研究显示，微调可能让大语言模型记忆并召回受版权保护的书籍段落，凸显训练数据记忆带来的版权与安全风险。该工作引发关于如何在对齐中降低记忆、并确保版权合规的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/79c4f2e462f608a0d1288e2077b9cb8f16eace838248e17b64c0b7715f1af4d2/cauchy221/Alignment-Whack-a-Mole-Code' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/79c4f2e462f608a0d1288e2077b9cb8f16eace838248e17b64c0b7715f1af4d2/cauchy221/Alignment-Whack-a-Mole-Code' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dillo 3.3.0 arrives with updates focused on lean footprint and modern web compatibility, continuing the ultralight browser niche. It remains appealing for embedded devices and privacy-minded users despite limited features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dillo 3.3.0 发布，强调保持极小体积并提升对现代网页的兼容性，延续了这款极简浏览器的定位。对于嵌入式设备和注重隐私的用户仍具吸引力，尽管功能有限。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.uscourts.gov/data-news/judiciary-news/2026/04/23/bankruptcies-increase-119-percent' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bankruptcies increase 11.9 percent</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47938756' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bankruptcies rose 11.9% year over year, signaling ongoing financial stress for individuals and small businesses amid macro headwinds. The uptick has implications for credit markets, consumer spending, and the resilience of the broader economy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>破产案件同比增长 11.9%，显示个人和小企业在宏观经济压力下持续承压。此趋势可能影响信贷市场、消费者支出与经济韧性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>臭味婴儿问题</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47950070' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Disposable diapers addressed odor by redesigning absorbency, materials, and containment, fueling wide adoption over cloth alternatives. The story also weighs environmental costs and the rapid growth of the diaper market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次性尿布通过改进吸收性、材料和封控设计解决了“臭味”问题，从而推动了相对于布尿布的广泛采用。文章也讨论了环境成本与尿布产业的快速扩张。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47966254' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://peterdohertys.website/blog-posts/full-text-search-w-duckdb.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Full-Text Search with DuckDB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 的全文检索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47966254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47966254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47966254' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to enabling full-text search with DuckDB, outlining the setup, indexing strategies, and performance considerations. The piece demonstrates how embedded analytics databases can provide rich search capabilities without requiring separate search engines, useful for data apps and notebooks. It also notes caveats compared to dedicated search systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在 DuckDB 中实现全文检索的实用指南，涵盖设置、索引策略及性能考量。展示了嵌入式分析数据库在无需独立搜索引擎的情况下提供丰富检索能力，对数据应用和笔记本场景尤为有用，同时也提醒了相对于专门的搜索系统的局限。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47977450' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47977450' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nHwxV0Sd9V8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock cameras keep telling police a man who doesn&#x27;t have a warrant has a warrant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 摄像头反复告知警方，一名无逮捕令的男子被错误标记为有逮捕令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977450' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video shows Flock cameras repeatedly flag a man as having a warrant, despite no warrant in reality. This highlights the risks of automated surveillance tools misidentifying individuals, potentially leading to wrongful police action and civil-liberties concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频显示 Flock 的摄像系统反复将一名无逮捕令的男子错误标记为有逮捕令。这凸显了自动化监控工具在识别个人时可能产生的误判，带来潜在的权利侵害与滥用风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47957624' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that Zig&#x27;s design—simplicity, cross-language ergonomics, and low-level control—should attract functional programmers. It suggests exploring Zig as a practical tool for FP workflows and systems programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文认为 Zig 的简洁设计、跨语言的易用性与底层控制能力，值得函数式编程者关注。它建议将 Zig 作为提升函数式编程工作流与系统编程的实用工具进行尝试。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47978834' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47978834' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/microsoft/lib0xc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lib0xc: A set of C standard library-adjacent APIs for safer systems programming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lib0xc：一组更安全的 C 标准库相关 API，面向更安全的系统编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47978834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47978834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47978834' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lib0xc provides a set of C standard library-adjacent APIs that emphasize safety for systems programming, with bounds-checked routines and safer defaults to reduce memory-safety risks while staying compatible with existing C code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lib0xc 提供一组接近 C 标准库的更安全 API，聚焦在边界检查和更安全的默认行为，帮助降低系统编程中的内存安全风险，同时尽量保持与现有代码的兼容性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5889945d833f61e1ccc2e73f9097e22e4da7d283735bc510aa07893833d19da4/microsoft/lib0xc' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5889945d833f61e1ccc2e73f9097e22e4da7d283735bc510aa07893833d19da4/microsoft/lib0xc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Arc Pro B70 review finds competitive performance for professional workloads, with strengths in 3D rendering and AI tasks, while noting drivers and software ecosystem as ongoing caveats. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Arc Pro B70 在专业工作负载方面表现具竞争力，尤其是在 3D 渲染与 AI 任务上，但驱动与软件生态仍需改善。此次评测让英特尔在工作站级显卡领域成为对抗对手的可行选择。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47918961' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47918961' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://robbowen.digital/wrote-about/abandoned-side-projects/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s OK to abandon your side-project (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>放弃副项目也没关系</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47918961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47918961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47918961' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dropping a side project can be a healthy, pragmatic choice when it no longer serves your goals. It frees time for higher-priority work and prevents sunk-cost traps, while still offering lessons learned from the attempt.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>放弃一个副项目往往是成熟的选择，能让你把精力集中在更重要的目标上，避免继续陷入已投入的成本陷阱。即使放弃，也能从尝试中汲取有价值的经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://robbowen.digital/assets/img/cards/abandoned-side-projects.png' data-full='https://robbowen.digital/assets/img/cards/abandoned-side-projects.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47936312' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/trycua/cua' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Drive any macOS app in the background without stealing the cursor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在后台驱动任意 macOS 应用且不抢光标</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47936312' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN introduces Cua, a macOS tool that lets you drive any app in the background without taking over the cursor. It enables smoother multi-app workflows and automation scenarios where focus management is a constraint.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 介绍的 Cua 是一个 macOS 工具，允许在后台控制任意应用且不抢占光标，提升多任务与自动化场景的工作流畅度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/eba866988a504a7f1d8bbb826a205b0bf1d331dae36b661df492bf06973eff71/trycua/cua' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/eba866988a504a7f1d8bbb826a205b0bf1d331dae36b661df492bf06973eff71/trycua/cua' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47972213' data-tags='Security,Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47972213' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.canonical.com/#/incident/KNms6QK9ewuzz-7xUsPsNylV20jEt5kyKsd8A-3ptQEHpOd8VQ40ZQs-KD81fboQXeGZB94okNHdHBGlCv58Sw==' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canonical/Ubuntu have been under DDoS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Canonical/Ubuntu 遭遇超过15小时的 DDoS 攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47972213' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canonical and Ubuntu infrastructure faced a sustained DDoS attack lasting more than 15 hours, disrupting services and user access. The incident underscores the need for robust DDoS defenses and incident response in high-visibility open-source ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canonical 与 Ubuntu 的基础设施遭受持续超过15小时的 DDoS 攻击，影响服务与用户访问。事件凸显在高可见性开源生态中部署强大防护和应急响应能力的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47966625' data-tags='Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47966625' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbos.dev/blog/benchmarking-workflow-execution-scalability-on-postgres' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does Postgres Scale?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres 能否扩展？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47966625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47966625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47966625' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A benchmarking post measures workflow execution scalability on PostgreSQL, exploring how well it handles growing complexity and parallelism. The results suggest PostgreSQL can scale for certain workloads with careful design, but may hit limits without specialized architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇基准测试文章评估 PostgreSQL 在工作流执行方面的扩展性，探讨其在日益复杂和并行化的任务中的表现。结论显示，在合适的设计下 PostgreSQL 可以在部分工作负载上实现可观的扩展性，但遇到极端规模时可能需要更专门的架构或工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/672411cbf038560468c9e68f/69ea2ef1c3fc50d09e3def99_Workflow-Execution-on-Postgres-Benchmark.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/672411cbf038560468c9e68f/69ea2ef1c3fc50d09e3def99_Workflow-Execution-on-Postgres-Benchmark.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47936347' data-tags='Security,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47936347' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aisle.com/blog/aisle-discovers-38-critical-security-vulnerabilities-in-healthcare-software-used-by-100000-providers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AISLE Discovers 38 CVEs in OpenEMR Healthcare Software</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AISLE 在 OpenEMR 医疗软件中发现 38 个 CVE 漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47936347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47936347' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers AISLE disclosed 38 CVEs in OpenEMR, including critical flaws that could enable exploitation. The widely used healthcare software powering thousands of providers underscores the importance of timely patching and secure software supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究公司 AISLE 在 OpenEMR 医疗软件中发现 38 个 CVE 漏洞，其中多处具有关键严重性，可能导致被利用。OpenEMR 被大量医疗机构使用，此事凸显了及时修补和保障医疗软件供应链安全的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aisle.com/api/media/file/OpenEMR%20-%20SOME%20post-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://aisle.com/api/media/file/OpenEMR%20-%20SOME%20post-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47945955' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rip.so' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Rip.so – a graveyard for dead internet things</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rip.so：死网遗产的墓园</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47945955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47945955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47945955' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rip.so serves as a cemetery for defunct internet services and artifacts, curating links, projects, and data that vanished over time. It highlights how quickly digital ecosystems decay and the value of archiving internet history for researchers and enthusiasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rip.so 提供一个专门存放已消失的互联网服务与遗存的“墓园”，帮助研究者与怀旧者访问‘消逝的网络’遗迹。此举凸显了对技术史的记录和透明度的重要性。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comprehensive, open-access textbook on engineering thermodynamics is freely available online, spanning fundamentals, cycles, and practical applications. It can save students money and serve as a reliable reference for courses, labs, and self-study.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一部工程热力学的免费电子教材现已在线开放获取，涵盖基础、循环与实际应用等内容。为学生、教师和自学者提供了省钱且可靠的参考资料。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chromium-drift.pages.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How far behind is each major Chromium browser?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>各大 Chromium 浏览器的落后程度到底有多远？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47999006' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparative look at how far behind each major Chromium-based browser is from upstream Chromium in features, performance, and update cadence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对比各大 Chromium 基础浏览器在功能、性能和更新节奏方面与上游 Chromium 的差距。揭示对网页兼容性和用户感知现代性可能造成的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
