<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47948526' data-tags='DevOps,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47948526' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/EnterpriseDB/barman' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Barman – Backup and Recovery Manager for PostgreSQL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Barman：PostgreSQL 备份与恢复管理工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948526' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47948526' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Barman centralizes PostgreSQL backups and recovery, enabling remote streaming backups, PITR, and automated maintenance across multiple servers. It&#x27;s a mature option for DB admins seeking auditable, disaster-ready protection in PostgreSQL deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Barman 集中管理 PostgreSQL 的备份与恢复，支持远程流式备份、时间点恢复以及跨服务器的自动化运维，帮助运维团队实现可审计、灾难就绪的数据库保护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/40883045/3075a580-6c0a-11e9-86ec-15c10687b15f' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/40883045/3075a580-6c0a-11e9-86ec-15c10687b15f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47992338' data-tags='Space,Politics,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47992338' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cvgzk91leweo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A network smuggling Starlink tech into Iran to beat internet blackout</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>秘密网络通过走私 Starlink 设备进入伊朗以绕过互联网封锁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47992338' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clandestine network smuggled Starlink hardware into Iran to bypass a government internet blackout. The move highlights how satellite-based connectivity can undermine censorship, raising questions about regulatory controls and information access. It also shows the ongoing tension between tech access and state control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个秘密网络向伊朗走私 Starlink 设备，试图突破政府的互联网封锁。此举揭示卫星联网在规避审查方面的潜力，同时引发对监管、供应链及信息获取影响的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/8c14/live/20b8b580-44c8-11f1-ac78-2112837ce2aa.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/8c14/live/20b8b580-44c8-11f1-ac78-2112837ce2aa.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47964957' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47964957' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dotcl/dotcl' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dotcl: Common Lisp Implementation on .NET</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dotcl：在 .NET 上实现的 Common Lisp</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964957' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47964957' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dotcl brings Common Lisp to the .NET platform, enabling Lisp code to run on the CLR and interoperate with other .NET languages. This opens up Lisp’s dynamic features to .NET developers and enhances language interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dotcl 将 Common Lisp 引入 .NET 平台，使 Lisp 代码能在 CLR 上运行并与其他 .NET 语言互操作。这为 .NET 开发者带来 Lisp 的动态特性，并提升语言互操作性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/570d3af6152ac0e5f504adc9f7b03eff4ca2813ebb2f86b7d73f49d7ec5c8761/dotcl/dotcl' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/570d3af6152ac0e5f504adc9f7b03eff4ca2813ebb2f86b7d73f49d7ec5c8761/dotcl/dotcl' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47913406' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47913406' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.barwypowstania.pl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 1944 Warsaw Uprising, in Color</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1944 年华沙起义，彩色再现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47913406' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The project colorizes archival footage of the 1944 Warsaw Uprising, bringing new visual context to a pivotal WWII event. It highlights how modern AI and video projects intersect with history and media preservation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对 1944 年华沙起义档案影像进行彩色化，该项目为这一历史事件带来新的视觉语境，提升了历史与媒体保存的现代技术应用。展示了 AI 在历史记录中的作用与局限。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47992772' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.feld.me/posts/2026/04/open-source-does-not-imply-open-community/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open source does not imply open community</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源并不等同于开放社区</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47992772' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that open-source projects can still have closed or gated communities, highlighting governance, contributor inclusion, and decision-making opacity. The takeaway: open source ≠ open governance; communities matter for sustainability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出开源项目也可能存在封闭的或受限的社区治理现象，强调治理透明度、参与者包容性与决策过程的重要性。结论是开源并不等同于开放的治理体系，社区的结构影响长期可持续性。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN post demonstrates Apple’s Sharp running in the browser via ONNX Runtime Web. It showcases how browser-based inference can be feasible for certain ML workloads without server round-trips.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示 Apple 的 Sharp 如何通过 ONNX Runtime Web 在浏览器中运行。演示了在浏览器端进行机器学习推理在无需服务器往返的情况下的可行性。讨论了在 JavaScript 环境中运行非原生模型时的可访问性与性能取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dystroy.org/blog/box-to-save-memory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Box to save memory in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 中的 Box：节省内存的思路</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47878231' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how Box&lt;T&gt; can reduce memory usage in Rust in certain patterns, weighing heap allocation costs against layout improvements and potential performance gains. The guidance helps readers decide when boxing is beneficial or避免不必要的装箱.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在特定模式下，Box&lt;T&gt; 如何降低 Rust 的内存占用，权衡堆分配成本与布局优化及潜在性能收益，帮助读者判断何时值得使用 Box，何时应避免不必要的装箱。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/pgcentralfoundation/pgrx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pgrx: Build Postgres Extensions with Rust</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47899669' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pgrx provides a Rust-based framework for developing PostgreSQL extensions, leveraging Rust’s safety and performance. This lowers the barrier for writing robust, high-performance server extensions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pgrx 提供基于 Rust 的框架来开发 PostgreSQL 扩展，利用 Rust 的安全性与性能。它简化了扩展的构建、测试与部署，有望扩大 Rust 在数据库工具链中的应用。为编写高性能、健壮的数据库扩展降低了门槛。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that the agent harness belongs outside the sandbox to reduce the risk of sandbox escapes. It discusses architectural trade-offs and how separating trust boundaries can improve security and reliability for AI agents.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Eka 的机械爪让人感觉我们正迎来‘ChatGPT 时刻’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47955789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47955789' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47955789' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A robotic claw from Eka demonstrates AI-assisted manipulation, hinting at a future where robots interpret language models to guide physical tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Eka 的机器人爪展示了 AI 辅助的抓取与操作，暗示未来机器人可能借助语言模型来理解指令并完成复杂物理任务。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47977498' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47977498' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/05/amazon-stuck-with-months-of-repairs-after-drone-strikes-on-data-centers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS stops billing Middle East cloud customers as repairs to war damage drag on</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS 暂停对中东云服务客户计费，修复战争造成的损坏仍在进行中</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47977498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977498' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon Web Services paused billing for Middle East cloud customers due to ongoing repairs from war damage to data centers, extending the disruption for clients relying on regional services. The incident highlights how geopolitical conflict can ripple into cloud economics and service continuity, prompting questions about resilience and billing transparency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊云服务因战争造成的数据中心损坏仍在修复中，暂停对中东地区云服务客户的计费。此举暴露出地缘政治冲击对云服务、运营成本和计费透明度的影响，凸显对韧性和客户沟通的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2264958756-1024x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/GettyImages-2264958756-1024x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47906420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47906420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47906420' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that affordable hardware or services often deliver better value and design elegance than premium options, challenging consumer tech narratives. The piece looks at where price-performance tradeoffs outperform prestige brands and what this means for builders.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张经济实惠的硬件或服务往往提供更高性价比和更巧妙的设计，挑战了对豪华品牌的偏见。文章分析何时价格/性能的权衡超过品牌溢价，以及这对开发者意味着什么。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002938' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that modern text-based user interfaces often fail accessibility due to ambiguous layouts, keyboard traps, and screen-reader inconsistencies, calling for accessibility-first design principles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出现代文本用户界面在无障碍方面的不足，原因包括布局模糊、键盘陷阱和屏幕阅读器不一致，呼吁以无障碍优先的设计原则。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47913382' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forty years after the disaster, wildlife in and around Chernobyl demonstrates resilience and ecological shifts, offering nuanced insights into radiation&#x27;s long-term ecological effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>切尔诺贝利灾难四十年后，区域内外的野生动物展现出韧性与生态变迁，为放射性长期影响提供了更细致的解读。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47969288' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://labs.watchtowr.com/the-internet-is-falling-down-falling-down-falling-down-cpanel-whm-authentication-bypass-cve-2026-41940/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CPanel and WHM Authentication Bypass – CVE-2026-41940</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CPanel 与 WHM 身份验证绕过漏洞 CVE-2026-41940</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47969288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47969288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47969288' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CVE-2026-41940 describes an authentication bypass in CPanel/WHM, potentially allowing attackers to access privileged accounts. The advisory urges upgrading to patched versions and applying mitigations; administrators should rotate credentials and review access controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CPanel/WHM 的身份验证绕过漏洞 CVE-2026-41940 已披露，可能使攻击者获取特权账户权限。建议尽快升级到修补版本并应用缓解措施，同时检查访问控制和凭据轮换。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/a0/dc/a0dcbbe4-0ae7-4d7e-90f7-ebbc3a0f5a84/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/Group-8730--2-.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/a0/dc/a0dcbbe4-0ae7-4d7e-90f7-ebbc3a0f5a84/content/images/size/w1200/2026/04/Group-8730--2-.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47921610' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/a-new-type-of-neuroplasticity-rewires-the-brain-after-a-single-experience-20260424/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Behavioral timescale synaptic plasticity rewires the brain after an experience</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>行为时间尺度的突触可塑性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47921610' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new form of neuroplasticity rewires brain circuits after a single experience, revealing rapid mechanisms that underlie learning and memory. The finding has implications for AI-inspired neuroscience and understanding how experiences shape networks fast.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种新的突触可塑性形式在单次经历后就重塑大脑回路，揭示快速学习记忆的机制。该发现对受 AI 启发的神经科学研究及理解经验如何迅速塑造网络具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/NewNeuroplasticity-crMarkBelan_YaseminSaplakoglu-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/NewNeuroplasticity-crMarkBelan_YaseminSaplakoglu-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47930493' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47930493' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/wasm-is-not-quite-a-stack-machine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WASM is not quite a stack machine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WASM 并非完全的栈机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47930493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47930493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47930493' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WebAssembly isn’t a pure stack machine; it uses an operand stack for expressions but relies on explicit locals, typed values, and structured control flow. This hybrid model informs how compilers, tooling, and runtimes optimize and debug Wasm modules. Understanding the nuance helps engineers design better performance and tooling strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WebAssembly 并非完全的栈机；它在表达式上使用操作数栈，但依赖显式局部变量、类型化值和结构化控制流。这一混合模型影响编译器、工具链与运行时在优化与调试 Wasm 模块时的策略。理解这一差异有助于在性能和工具开发上做出更明智的选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/wasm-is-not-quite-a-stack-machine/og.png' data-full='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/wasm-is-not-quite-a-stack-machine/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47975592' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47975592' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/whohas/whohas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Whohas – Command-line utility for cross-distro, cross-repository package search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>whohas：跨发行版、跨仓库的软件包搜索命令行工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47975592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47975592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47975592' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>whohas is a CLI tool to search packages across Linux distributions and repositories, streamlining compatibility checks for multi-distro environments. It helps developers quickly locate where a package exists and what version is available, reducing dependency guesswork.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>whohas 是一个命令行工具，用于跨 Linux 发行版和仓库搜索软件包，简化多发行环境下的兼容性检查。它帮助开发者快速定位软件包的位置与可用版本，降低依赖猜测成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7faee107133727c56d2e417d33b10b454cb243f3581b8f7cd5294c501be4d0e6/whohas/whohas' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7faee107133727c56d2e417d33b10b454cb243f3581b8f7cd5294c501be4d0e6/whohas/whohas' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47954638' data-tags='Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47954638' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lina.sh/blog/ddos-honeypot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I accidentally made law enforcement shut down their fake honeypot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不小心让执法部门关闭了他们的假蜜罐</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47954638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47954638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47954638' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blogger recounts accidentally causing law-enforcement&#x27;s fake honeypot to be shut down, sharing insights into the risks and legal-gray areas of threat-hunting tooling. The post offers practical lessons on responsible disclosure and civility in cybersecurity experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博主讲述自己意外导致执法部门的假蜜罐被关闭的经历，探讨威胁猎杀工具的风险与法律边界。文中提出在网络安全实验中实现负责任披露与克制的实用经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lina.sh/assets/blog/cyberzap-pricing.png' data-full='https://lina.sh/assets/blog/cyberzap-pricing.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47918891' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47918891' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apod.nasa.gov/apod/ap260427.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can You Find the Comet?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能找到彗星吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47918891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47918891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47918891' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The feature highlights a comet visible in the sky and offers practical tips for observers on when and how to spot it. It underscores how approachable astronomy can be with public imagery and gentle guidance. For hobbyists, it’s a reminder that celestial fireworks are within reach with a clear sky and a little patience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该日的天文图片鼓励观星爱好者在夜空中寻找彗星，并给出观测条件和时间的建议。通过易懂的指导，读者能更易上手天文观测。对于爱好者，这提醒我们在晴朗夜空与一点耐心下就能看到天文奇观。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.derekthompson.org/p/why-do-richer-dads-spend-more-time' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>American Dads Became the Parents Their Fathers Never Were</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国爸爸已成为比父亲辈更愿意花时间陪伴子女的父母</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47965027' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Derek Thompson argues that richer dads are spending more time with their children than their own fathers did, reflecting debt of time and resources across generations. The piece analyzes how wealth, work flexibility, and social norms shape parenting, with implications for child development and family policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Derek Thompson 指出，富裕阶层的父亲花在陪伴子女上的时间增多，超过了他们父辈的水平。文章分析财富、工作灵活性和社会规范如何塑造育儿方式，并对儿童发展与家庭政策产生影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47944864' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hardenedbsd.org/article/shawn-webb/2026-04-26/hardenedbsd-officially-radicle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HardenedBSD Is Now Officially on Radicle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HardenedBSD 正式接入 Radicle</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47944864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47944864' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47944864' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HardenedBSD has officially joined Radicle, bringing its security-hardened codebase to the decentralized, peer-to-peer collaboration network. The integration enables HardenedBSD developers to collaborate without relying on centralized services, leveraging Radicle’s distributed version control and verification workflow. This move highlights growing interest in decentralized development tooling for security-focused projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HardenedBSD 已正式加入 Radicle，将其安全强化代码库带入去中心化的点对点协作网络。此次集成允许开发者在不依赖中心化服务的情况下协作，利用 Radicle 的分布式版本控制与验证工作流。这一举动凸显了安全导向项目对去中心化开发工具的关注日增。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hardenedbsd.org/sites/default/files/2014-08-21_logo_small_03_0_0.png' data-full='https://hardenedbsd.org/sites/default/files/2014-08-21_logo_small_03_0_0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pagetable.com/?p=1854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Running Adobe&#x27;s 1991 PostScript Interpreter in the Browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在浏览器中运行 Adobe 的 1991 年 PostScript 解释器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47973755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47973755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47973755' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project ports Adobe&#x27;s 1991 PostScript interpreter to run in the browser, allowing faithful rendering of legacy PostScript documents without server-side tooling. It highlights the challenges of porting a vintage interpreter to modern environments and the performance/precision considerations involved. The effort underscores the value of emulation for digital preservation and retro computing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个将 Adobe 1991 年 PostScript 解释器移植到浏览器的项目，使老旧的 PostScript 文档能够在客户端渲染，避免对服务器端运行时的依赖。展示了把历史解释器带入现代运行时的挑战以及性能与精度的权衡。此工作凸显了在数字保存和复古计算领域，浏览器端仿真的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pagetable.com/docs/retro-ps/retro-ps.png' data-full='https://www.pagetable.com/docs/retro-ps/retro-ps.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47924188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47924188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada launches its first sovereign wealth fund to manage natural resource revenues and diversify government investment. Backed by policy leadership from figures like Carney, the fund aims to stabilize revenues and invest in long-term national projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大设立首个主权财富基金，旨在管理自然资源收入并多元化政府投资。由卡尼等领衔的政策领导力推动，基金目标在于稳定财政收入并为长期国家项目择优投资。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47974307' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47974307' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Embedded Rust or C firmware? Lessons from an industrial microcontroller use case</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>工业微控制器用例中的嵌入式 Rust 与 C 固件取舍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47974307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 142</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47974307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47974307' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study contrasts using Rust versus C for embedded firmware on an industrial MCU. It weighs safety, determinism, and resource constraints against tooling and ecosystem maturity, offering practical lessons on when Rust&#x27;s memory safety is worth the trade-offs. It also covers integration with existing toolchains and debugging considerations in real-time environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项用例研究对工业微控制器上的嵌入式固件，比较 Rust 与 C 的实现。在权衡安全性、确定性和资源约束的同时，讨论工具链成熟度对选择的影响，并给出在实时环境中何时采用 Rust 的实际建议。还涉及与现有工具链的集成及调试注意事项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mobeigi.com/blog/security/security-through-obscurity-is-not-bad/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Security through obscurity is not bad</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安全性并非来自暴露：隐藏策略也有价值</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47997486' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that obscurity, used judiciously, can complement strong security practices rather than replace them. It discusses how limited visibility can reduce attack surfaces, protect IP, and buy time against adversaries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张在恰当时机下的隐藏/保密可以与强安全措施互补，而不是取代它们。文中指出有限的可见性可降低攻击面、保护知识产权，并为防御赢得时间。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/01/uber-wants-to-turn-its-millions-of-drivers-into-a-sensor-grid-for-self-driving-companies/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uber wants to turn its drivers into a sensor grid for self-driving companies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Uber 想把司机变成自驾传感器网</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47987333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47987333' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47987333' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Uber envisions turning its driver fleet into a distributed sensor grid for autonomous vehicle developers, monetizing real-world data and test trips. The plan could accelerate AV iteration but raises questions about driver consent, data governance, and regulatory risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Uber 正在设想把司机队伍变成分布式传感器网，用于自动驾驶开发商的数据采集与测试并实现商业化。此举可能加速自动驾驶的迭代，但也引发对司机同意、数据治理和监管风险的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47949794' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47949794' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/digest/atoll-islands-sea-level-rise-fungi' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>To Restore an Island Paradise, Add Fungi</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为修复岛屿天堂，添加真菌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47949794' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yale Environment Research highlights that introducing fungi can improve soil formation, vegetation, and resilience on atolls facing sea-level rise. By boosting nutrient cycling and soil stability, fungi offer a practical tool for climate adaptation in vulnerable island ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>耶鲁环境研究强调，在面临海平面上升的环礁岛屿上添加真菌，可以改善土壤形成、植被覆盖与生态韧性。通过提升养分循环和土壤稳定性，真菌为脆弱岛屿环境的气候适应提供了切实可行的工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Palmyra-Atoll_USFWS.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1777370036&amp;s=7780e4a4b6cf9acd95ab9f441eb39d2f' data-full='https://yale-threesixty.transforms.svdcdn.com/production/Palmyra-Atoll_USFWS.jpg?w=1200&amp;q=60&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=max&amp;dm=1777370036&amp;s=7780e4a4b6cf9acd95ab9f441eb39d2f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47990708' data-tags='Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47990708' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hnup.date/hn-sota' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: State of the Art of Coding Models, According to Hacker News Commenters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：据 Hacker News 评论者所评的编码模型现状</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47990708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47990708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47990708' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This Show HN compiles Hacker News commenters&#x27; views to map the current state-of-the-art in coding models. It highlights which models are favored for code tasks, trade-offs between speed and accuracy, and community sentiment shaping practical tool choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此 Show HN 汇总 Hacker News 评论者的观点，勾勒当前编码模型的现状，揭示在代码任务中的偏好模型、速度与准确度的权衡，以及社区对工具选择的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-04-usindian-space-mission-extreme-subsidence.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US–Indian space mission maps extreme subsidence in Mexico City</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美印太空任务绘制墨西哥城极端下沉图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47974682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47974682' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47974682' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US-Indian space mission mapped extreme subsidence in Mexico City, highlighting rapid ground sinking driven by groundwater extraction and urban growth. The findings underline the vulnerability of infrastructure and the need for sustainable water management and adaptive urban planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美印合作的太空任务绘制出墨西哥城的极端下沉区域，揭示因地下水抽取和快速城市化导致的地面下沉。研究结果凸显基建脆弱性，强调需要加强水资源管理和宜居的城市规划来降低风险。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mirandaheath.website/static/oss_burnout_report_mh_25.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A report on burnout in open source software communities (2025) [pdf]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47981669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47981669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47981669' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>2025 report on burnout in open source communities reveals sustained fatigue among maintainers and contributors, driven by burnout, high release tempo, and funding gaps. It argues for sustainable governance, paid maintainers, and healthier collaboration practices to keep OSS healthy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年的开源社区倦怠报告揭示维护者与贡献者长期疲惫，原因包括高密度的发行节奏和资金不足。报告主张通过可持续治理、付费维护者以及更健康的协作方式来维护开源生态的长期活力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jeffschomay.com/letting-ai-play-my-game' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Letting AI play my game – building an agentic test harness to help play-testing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 AI 玩我的游戏：构建一个具代理能力的测试框架以辅助试玩测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47947525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47947525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47947525' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental harness lets AI autonomously play a game, surfacing bugs and player-experience insights that human testers might miss. The setup discusses how to measure agentic play, handle non-determinism, and translate findings into test and design improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个实验性框架让 AI 自动试玩游戏，能暴露人类测试难以发现的漏洞和体验问题。该方法涉及如何衡量代理行为、处理非确定性，并把发现转化为测试与设计改进。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://evilgeniuslabs.ca/blog/good-developers-learn-to-program-not-a-language' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Good developers learn to program. Most courses teach a language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>好开发者会学会编程。大多数课程教的是一种语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47981995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47981995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47981995' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Great developers are defined by their ability to think like programmers, not by knowing a single language. The piece argues that most courses teach syntax rather than core concepts, urging learners to focus on problem-solving, abstraction, and cross-language practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>优秀开发者的核心在于具备编程思维，而非掌握某一种语言。文章指出多数课程教授的是语法而非核心概念，建议学习者专注于问题解决、抽象能力以及跨语言练习。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47972659' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/intel/auto-round' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Advanced Quantization Algorithm for LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面向大语言模型的高级量化算法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47972659' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Auto-Round introduces a sophisticated quantization approach for large language models, preserving accuracy while shrinking model size and speeding up inference. The technique promises meaningful efficiency gains for CPU/GPU deployment and lowers the cost of running large models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Auto-Round 提出了一种面向大语言模型的高级量化方法，在不显著损失精度的前提下缩小模型规模并加速推理。该技术有望在 CPU/GPU 部署中带来显著的效率提升，降低大模型的运营成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/67df602be56917fc94933f6ec25306fc6999e4881d962c672a5b8aa5795a3ca2/intel/auto-round' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/67df602be56917fc94933f6ec25306fc6999e4881d962c672a5b8aa5795a3ca2/intel/auto-round' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47907175' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47907175' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/apr/15/hidden-treasures-spanish-archaeologists-discover-trove-of-ancient-shipwrecks-in-bay-of-gibraltar' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spanish archaeologists discover trove of ancient shipwrecks in Bay of Gibraltar</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙考古学家在直布罗陀湾发现大量古代船骸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47907175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47907175' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spanish archaeologists have uncovered a trove of ancient shipwrecks in the Bay of Gibraltar, expanding our view of maritime history. The discoveries leverage modern underwater methods—like sonar mapping and remotely operated equipment—to document and preserve the wrecks. The find adds rich data for historians and may inform ancient trade network studies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙考古学家在直布罗陀湾发现了一批古代船只残骸，扩展了对海上贸易历史的认知。此次发现依托现代水下探测手段，如声纳制图和远程操作设备，对船骸进行记录与保护。研究成果为历史学家提供新的证据，也可能揭示当时的贸易网络。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e6061b1b8fb08d4492a358f75035d4a4727ebe7d/6_0_1500_1200/master/1500.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=61659f4be83d67a0a51559f40c1bcf01' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/e6061b1b8fb08d4492a358f75035d4a4727ebe7d/6_0_1500_1200/master/1500.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=61659f4be83d67a0a51559f40c1bcf01' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47998449' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://citizenlab.ca/research/uncovering-global-telecom-exploitation-by-covert-surveillance-actors/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bad Connection: Global telecom exploitation by covert surveillance actors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球电信被隐秘监控行动者利用的安全漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47998449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47998449' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A global study reveals covert surveillance actors repeatedly exploit telecom infrastructure to monitor people, challenging privacy and security norms. The report maps tactics, targets, and calls for stronger safeguards and accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球性研究揭示隐蔽监控者反复利用电信基础设施进行监控，威胁隐私与网络安全。报告梳理了攻击手段和目标，并呼吁加强防护与问责。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://citizenlab.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/1_Bad-Connection-01.jpg' data-full='https://citizenlab.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/1_Bad-Connection-01.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47927526' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An outage hit the NPM registry, interrupting package publishing and installs for JavaScript/Node developers. The incident highlights the fragility of centralized ecosystems and the importance of robust incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NPM 注册表发生宕机，影响 JavaScript/Node 开发者的包发布与安装。事件凸显集中式生态的脆弱性以及对强健应急响应的需求。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47977990' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how underdrawings help align annotations with final drawings and diagrams, outlining workflows for overlaying text and numbers to improve documentation accuracy, revision control, and QA.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 how underdrawings 如何帮助将注释与最终图纸对齐，概述在图解上叠加文本与数字的工作流程，从而提升文档准确性、版本控制和质量检查。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47915023' data-tags='Science,Space,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47915023' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-04-deep-antarctic-ice-cosmic-strange.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deep under Antarctic ice, a long-predicted cosmic whisper breaks through</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>深埋南极冰层之下，长期预期的宇宙低语终于浮现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47915023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47915023' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47915023' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers report a detection of a long-predicted cosmic signal deep beneath the Antarctic ice, validating a key theoretical piece of astrophysics. The breakthrough demonstrates the power of polar detectors for studying high-energy phenomena and could open new multi-messenger opportunities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者在南极冰层深处探测到长期预测的宇宙信号，验证了天体物理学中的一个关键理论。此发现展示了极地探测在研究高能现象方面的潜力，并可能开启新的多信使观测机会。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/2026/antarctic-experiment-d.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/2026/antarctic-experiment-d.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47995757' data-tags='Science,Health,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47995757' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sciencex.com/news/2026-04-coffee-doesnt-key-biological-pathway.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nuclear receptor 4A1 linked to health effects of coffee: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>咖啡与健康：NR4A1 与咖啡健康效应的关联研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47995757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47995757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47995757' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study links nuclear receptor NR4A1 to observed health effects from coffee consumption, suggesting molecular pathways that influence metabolism and inflammation. The finding helps explain why coffee impacts people differently and could guide personalized nutrition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现核受体 NR4A1 与咖啡摄入的健康效应相关，提示可能影响代谢和炎症的分子通路。研究有助于解释个体对咖啡反应的差异，并为个性化营养提供线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/coffee-doesnt-just-wak.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/coffee-doesnt-just-wak.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47969142' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47969142' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/04/brain-scans-individual-versus-group.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Group averages obscure how an individual&#x27;s brain controls behavior: study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>群体平均掩盖个人大脑对行为的控制方式：研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47969142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47969142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47969142' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study finds that averaging brain data across groups hides how an individual brain maps to behavior. Individual-level analyses reveal patterns that group means miss, challenging one-size-fits-all interpretations. The work supports personalized brain models and cautions against over-reliance on group averages in neuroscience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项研究发现，将大脑数据按组平均会掩盖个体脑部如何映射到行为的方式。个体级分析揭示了群体均值所忽略的模式，挑战了“一个方案适用于所有人”的解读。研究强调需要建立个性化的大脑模型，并警示不要过度依赖群体平均数据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/04/brain-scans-individual-versus-group/_jcr_content/cq:socialfeaturedimage.coreimg.jpeg/1777295241330/Menon-brain-scans.jpeg' data-full='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/04/brain-scans-individual-versus-group/_jcr_content/cq:socialfeaturedimage.coreimg.jpeg/1777295241330/Menon-brain-scans.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matduggan.com/if-i-could-make-my-own-github/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If I could make my own GitHub</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47962269' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores building a GitHub-like platform from scratch, weighing open-source, self-hosting, feature scope, and governance trade-offs. It offers practical design considerations for developers aiming to create developer tooling with community ownership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨从零构建一个类似 GitHub 的平台，权衡开源、自托管、功能边界和治理等要点。提供面向开发者工具的实际设计建议，强调社区所有权与可持续性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47941931' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47941931' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.amusingplanet.com/2022/08/london-to-calcutta-by-bus.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>London to Calcutta by Bus (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从伦敦到加尔各答的公路之旅（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47941931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47941931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47941931' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2022 overland journey by bus from London to Calcutta documents the logistical and border-crossing challenges of long-distance travel by land. The piece contrasts slow, overland travel with the ubiquity of flying, highlighting route choices, time costs, and intercultural encounters along the way.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2022年一场横跨欧洲至亚洲的公路巴士旅程记录了长途陆路旅行的后勤与边境挑战。文章对比了陆路旅行与飞行的差异，强调路线选择、时间成本以及沿途的跨文化体验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/sambigeara/pollen' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Pollen – distributed WASM runtime, no control plane, single binary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pollen：分布式 WASM 运行时，无控制平面，单一二进制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pollen provides a distributed WebAssembly runtime in a single binary with no central control plane, aiming to reduce orchestration overhead and improve portability across nodes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pollen 提供一个单一二进制、无中心控制平面的分布式 WASM 运行时，旨在降低编排开销并提升节点间的可移植性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伟大抽象背后的隐藏成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48002607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48002607' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Turns a critical eye to high-level abstractions, showing how they can hide costs like performance overhead, debugging complexity, and maintenance burdens. Encourages mindful design choices that expose true costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批判性地审视高层抽象，揭示它们可能隐藏的成本，如性能开销、调试复杂性和维护负担。倡导在设计时对真实成本有清晰认识并做出谨慎选择。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47927011' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47927011' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idlemachines.co.uk/essays/softmax' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Softmax, can you derive the Jacobian? And should you care?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Softmax：能推导雅可比矩阵吗？是否值得关心？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47927011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47927011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47927011' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post derives the Jacobian of the Softmax function and discusses its relevance for practical machine learning. While the Jacobian is dense and coupled, understanding it helps diagnose gradient flow in multi-class models and informs numerical implementation choices. The takeaway is that Softmax’s interactions across outputs matter more than they appear at first glance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章推导了 Softmax 的雅可比矩阵，并讨论其在实际机器学习中的作用。虽然雅可比矩阵很稠密且耦合，但理解它有助于诊断多分类模型的梯度流动，并为数值实现提供指引。要点在于输出之间的耦合关系比初看时更为重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://idlemachines.co.uk/api/og/essay?title=Softmax%2C%20can%20you%20really%20derive%20the%20Jacobian%3F%20And%20should%20you%20care%3F' data-full='https://idlemachines.co.uk/api/og/essay?title=Softmax%2C%20can%20you%20really%20derive%20the%20Jacobian%3F%20And%20should%20you%20care%3F' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rocky-data/rocky' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Rocky – Rust SQL engine with branches, replay, column lineage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Rocky——具分支、回放与列级血缘的 Rust SQL 引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47935246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47935246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47935246' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rocky is a Rust-based SQL engine with support for branches, query replay, and column lineage. It aims to be embeddable and auditable, enabling reproducible results and fine-grained data provenance for data pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rocky 是一个用 Rust 编写的 SQL 引擎，支持分支、查询回放和列级血缘。目标是可嵌入、可审计，适合需要可重复结果和细粒度数据血缘的数据管线场景。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47986136' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study suggests refusals in language models are mediated by a single direction in input space, offering a new lens on prompt design and alignment risks. This could guide safer prompt engineering and model tuning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明语言模型的拒绝输出受输入空间中一个单一方向的调控，为提示设计与对齐风险提供了新视角，或将引导更安全的提示工程与模型调优。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47949616' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47949616' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(287) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/what-can-we-gain-by-losing-infinity-20260429/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What can we gain by losing infinity?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们能通过失去无限获得什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47949616' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how embracing finitary approaches or truncating infinite concepts can yield new mathematical or computational insights. The piece ties abstract ideas to practical gains in computation, physics, or philosophy, showing that shedding the ideal of infinity can lead to usable approximations or new theories. It emphasizes the value of finite models in tackling real-world problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在将无限概念简化或接受有限化时，如何带来新的数学或计算洞见。文章将抽象理念与计算、物理或哲学的实际应用联系起来，展示放弃无限理想能带来可用的近似或新理论，强调有限模型在现实问题中的价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Anti-Infinity-cr-Kristina-Armitage-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Anti-Infinity-cr-Kristina-Armitage-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47997056' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47997056' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(288) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simpleobservability.com/docs/alert-driven-monitoring' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alert-driven monitoring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>基于告警驱动的监控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47997056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47997056' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alert-Driven Monitoring describes a monitoring approach where alerts guide the instrumentation and response workflows. It argues for designing actionable alerts with clear runbooks and automation to reduce noise and speed incident containment. The concept complements existing metrics dashboards by focusing on what actually requires action.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>告警驱动的监控将告警作为驱动，将监控的编排和响应流程引导。主张以可操作的告警、清晰的处置手册与自动化为设计核心，以减少噪声并提升事件处置速度。该理念与现有的指标仪表板互补，聚焦于真正需要采取行动的场景。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1066156/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A more efficient implementation of Shor&#x27;s algorithm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>更高效的 Shor 算法实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47972114' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A more efficient implementation of Shor&#x27;s algorithm advances quantum factoring, potentially requiring fewer qubits or tolerating less error. The development nudges the threshold for breaking certain cryptosystems and underscores the urgency of post-quantum cryptography research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shor 算法的更高效实现提升了量子因子分解的资源效率，可能用更少的量子比特或更低的误差完成运算。对技术人员而言，这意味着需要更快推进后量子密码学研究。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47971768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47971768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple has announced that Mac Studio and Mac Mini will be in short supply for months, signaling ongoing supply-chain constraints despite strong demand. The shortage could affect buyers, developers, and upgrade plans, and hints at broader pressures in the semiconductor and logistics ecosystem. It may impact pacing of new product workflows and budgeting for teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布 Mac Studio 和 Mac Mini 将在数月内短缺，表明需求强劲下供应链仍然受限。缺货可能影响买家、开发者及升级计划，也反映出半导体与物流生态的更广泛压力，或将影响团队的工作节奏与预算分配。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vice.com/en/article/meet-the-mushroom-that-make-people-have-the-exact-same-hallucination/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Mushroom That Makes People Have the Exact Same Hallucination</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让人产生完全相同幻觉的蘑菇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47918657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47918657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47918657' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A psychedelic mushroom is said to produce strikingly similar visions across different people, prompting questions about shared perceptual effects and the neuroscience of psychedelics. The piece weighs anecdotal reports against scientific explanations and safety considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称这种致幻蘑菇能让不同个体产生高度一致的幻觉。文章探讨了在致幻剂作用下，共同知觉现象如何产生，以及这对大脑处理信息的启示，同时考虑科学解释与安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hpcwire.com/2026/02/05/what-is-z-angle-memory-and-why-is-intel-developing-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is Z-Angle Memory and why is Intel developing it?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47972922' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Z-Angle Memory is a novel memory concept being explored by Intel to improve data access patterns for HPC workloads. It hints at potential gains in bandwidth, latency, and energy efficiency, while outlining engineering challenges to bring it to production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Z-Angle Memory 是英特尔在探索的新型内存理念，旨在改善高性能计算工作负载的数据访问模式。其潜在收益包括带宽、延迟和能效的提升，同时也面临实现上的技术挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47939158' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Church_Rock_uranium_mill_spill' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Church Rock Uranium Mill Spill</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Church Rock 铀矿厂泄漏事故</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939158' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47939158' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1979 uranium mill spill at Church Rock released large amounts of radioactive waste into the desert; it&#x27;s one of the worst nuclear incidents in US history. It underscores legacy pollution and regulatory challenges in mining and energy sectors, with lessons for tech and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Church Rock 铀矿厂在 1979 年发生严重的放射性废料泄漏，成为美国历史上最严重的核事故之一。此事揭示了矿业与能源领域的遗留污染与监管挑战，对科技与政策具有长期影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/d/d5/United_Nuclear_Corporation_Church_Rock_Uranium_Mill.jpeg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/d/d5/United_Nuclear_Corporation_Church_Rock_Uranium_Mill.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://samizdat.dev/phantom-patch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Patch applies fake diffs from commit messages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>补丁从提交信息中应用伪造的差异</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47917250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47917250' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47917250' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The phantom patch concept demonstrates applying fake diffs inferred from commit messages, underscoring risks of relying on textual metadata for code changes and potential security concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>幻影补丁技术演示了基于提交信息推断并应用伪造差异的补丁工具，暴露了仅依赖文本元数据进行代码变更的风险及潜在安全隐患。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47995125' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/9921f2b5-c910-4cec-a50f-cad453935a1a' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Investors pile into clean energy as Iran war drives push for energy security</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在伊朗战争推动能源安全的背景下，投资者涌向清洁能源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47995125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47995125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47995125' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Investors are flocking to clean energy as geopolitical tensions push energy security higher on the agenda. The surge in funding spans renewables, storage, and grid resilience, with policy and market signals accelerating the transition. Geopolitics may expedite diversification away from fossil fuels and boost long-term climate and energy goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在地缘政治紧张局势推动能源安全议程之际，投资者正涌向清洁能源。资金涌向可再生能源、储能与电网韧性建设，政策与市场信号正在加速能源转型。地缘政治因素可能加速对化石燃料的多元化依赖，推动长期气候与能源目标的实现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F566a691b-057b-4598-8795-7b11a7d27b87.jpg?source=next-barrier-page' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F566a691b-057b-4598-8795-7b11a7d27b87.jpg?source=next-barrier-page' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
