<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47987304' data-tags='Science,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47987304' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zugzwang' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zugzwang</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zugzwang（棋局困境）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47987304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47987304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47987304' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zugzwang is a situation in which every legal move worsens the player&#x27;s position, a concept widely used in game theory and algorithmic decision problems. Understanding it helps diagnose suboptimal strategies in adversarial environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zugzwang 指的是在棋局中任何合法走法都会使持方处于不利局面的情形，广泛用于博弈论和算法决策问题。理解这一概念有助于分析对抗环境中的劣势决策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' data-full='https://en.wikipedia.org/static/images/icons/enwiki-25.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://retrocoding.net/windows-api-is-successful-cross-platform-api' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows API is Successful Cross-Platform API (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows API 已成为成功的跨平台 API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47992859' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Windows API has become a successful cross-platform API, reflecting how Windows interfaces and compatibility layers have become usable across different OSes. This trend affects how developers design cross-platform apps, reducing the need to rewrite for each platform and influencing porting strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows API 已成为一个成功的跨平台接口，表明通过兼容层在不同操作系统上使用 Windows 接口已成为现实。此趋势正在影响开发者的跨平台设计与移植策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47963868' data-tags='Design,Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47963868' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-1960s-art-school-experiment-that-redefined-creativity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 1960s art school experiment that redefined creativity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1960 年代美术学院的一场重新定义创造力的实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47963868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47963868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47963868' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece surveys a 1960s art-school experiment that reshaped how people think about creativity, likely involving interactive pedagogy and cross-disciplinary collaboration. It offers takeaways for design, education, and tech teams about nurturing experimentation and breaking intellectual boundaries. The lesson is that curricula can unlock new modes of creative thinking when teachers embrace risk and collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾了1960年代美术学院的一场实验，重新定义了人们对创造力的理解，往往涉及互动式教学与跨学科协作。为设计、教育乃至科技团队提供启示：在教师鼓励冒险与协作的条件下，课程能开启全新的创造力表达方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/evanklem/evanflow' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EvanFlow – A TDD driven feedback loop for Claude Code</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47916909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47916909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47916909' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EvanFlow uses TDD to drive feedback loop for Claude Code, enabling automated tests and regression checks. This approach helps steer prompt engineering and improve reliability of AI-generated code. It demonstrates how short, repeatable tests can guide model usage effectively.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EvanFlow 通过测试驱动开发推动 Claude Code 的反馈循环，支持自动化测试与回归检查。这种方法有助于引导提示工程并提升 AI 产出代码的可靠性，展示了如何用短小、可重复的测试来有效引导模型使用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47989154' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47989154' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://c3-lang.org/blog/unsigned-sizes-a-five-year-mistake/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unsigned sizes: A five year mistake</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无符号大小的五年误解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47989154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47989154' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47989154' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that using unsigned integers to represent sizes leads to subtle bugs, security holes, and confusing APIs, citing five years of accumulated issues. It advocates safer abstractions or bounds-aware APIs to prevent common off-by-one and wraparound errors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为用无符号整数表示大小容易引发细微的Bug、安全隐患与混乱的 API，并指出这些问题在五年内积累。文中倡议采用更安全的抽象或边界感知的 API，以避免越界和环绕等常见错误。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://c3-lang.org/logo.png' data-full='https://c3-lang.org/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Auto Polo is a historical experiment where drivers control cars on a polo field, blending early automotive tech with sport. The note covers its origins, rules, and why it stands as a curious footnote in the history of vehicles and games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自动马球是一项早期的实验性运动，驾驶员驾驶汽车在马球场上竞技，融合了汽车技术与体育趣味。文中回顾了起源、规则，以及其在汽车史与体育史中的有趣地位。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法院裁定第二修正案覆盖枪支部件——对制造枪支者有利</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47950377' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court ruling extends Second Amendment protections to firearm parts used to assemble weapons, signaling a shift in how DIY guns are regulated. The decision raises questions about serialization, regulation, and enforcement for component parts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院裁定第二修正案也覆盖用于组装枪支的部件，这将影响DIY 枪支的监管方式。该判决引发关于零部件序列化、监管与执法的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/aallan/vera' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vera: a programming language designed for machines to write</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vera：为机器编写代码而设计的编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47955118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47955118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47955118' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vera is a programming language aimed at machines writing code, exploring how automated generation can shape software development. Early experiments show potential for synthesis-driven workflows and new tooling paradigms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vera 是一门为“让机器去写代码”而设计的编程语言，研究自动化生成如何改变软件开发。早期实验显示在合成驱动的工作流和新工具范式方面的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The team reports significant cost reductions for running LLM workloads using Opus, outlining the approach and its trade-offs in accuracy vs. efficiency. The post provides practical guidance for teams optimizing inference budgets without sacrificing too much performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>团队在使用 Opus 的情况下显著降低了运行大型语言模型的成本，概述了方法与准确性与效率之间的权衡。文章为在不大幅牺牲性能的前提下优化推理预算的团队提供实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47999460' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(332) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8jNQDcpHc68' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I recreated the Apple Lisa computer inside an FPGA [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在 FPGA 中重现 Apple Lisa 计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999460' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47999460' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on project recreates the Apple Lisa architecture on an FPGA, offering a tangible tour of its hardware quirks and OS interactions. It provides nostalgia plus a practical view into how legacy systems can be prototyped today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过在 FPGA 上复现 Apple Lisa 的架构，展示了其硬件特点与与操作系统的交互。既有怀旧情怀，也展现了用现代现场可编程硬件原型化旧系统的可行性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47947318' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physics-informed coffee brewing tips explain how heat transfer, diffusion, and extraction time shape flavor, offering practical tweaks for grind size, water temperature, and brew duration. A practical read for coffee nerds seeking repeatable, tastier results.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过热传导、扩散和萃取时间等物理原理解释咖啡风味形成，并给出在研磨、水温和浸泡时间上的实用调整，帮助追求稳定且更美味的冲泡结果。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47944924' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany reportedly overtook the US in ammunition production capacity, driven by defense spending and manufacturing efficiencies. The shift could impact NATO readiness and supply chain dynamics in conflict scenarios and procurement planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国在弹药生产能力方面超过美国，这得益于国防支出和制造效率的提升。这一变化可能影响北约的备战、供应链动态以及相关采购策略。</p>

The note outlines the reasons behind the decision and hints at alternate plans, signaling where the event will land next. The move underscores how political and logistical factors shape global digital rights gatherings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RightsCon 2026 不在赞比亚举行的官方声明揭示了决定背后的原因，并暗示将有替代安排，指向下一步的举办地点。此举反映了政治与后勤因素如何影响全球数字权利大会的安排。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.augmentcode.com/blog/how-to-write-good-agents-dot-md-files' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A good AGENTS.md is a model upgrade. A bad one is worse than no docs at all</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47938417' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47938417' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47938417' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A well-crafted agents.md can improve AI agent reliability and maintainability, while sloppily documented agents can degrade understanding and safety. The post offers best practices for writing effective agents documentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，优秀的 agents.md 能提升 AI 代理的鲁棒性和维护性；相反，差的文档会带来误导和风险，并给架构带来负面影响。给出编写指南与最佳实践。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47949538' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47949538' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/apr/29/making-ai-chatbots-more-friendly-mistakes-support-false-beliefs-conspiracy-theories-study' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making AI chatbots friendly leads to mistakes and support of conspiracy theories</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 AI 聊天机器人更友好会导致错误并助长阴谋论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47949538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47949538' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study shows that making AI chatbots friendlier can increase user trust but also lead to mistakes and a higher chance of conspiracy-belief propagation. It warns designers to balance friendliness with accuracy and guardrails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现让 AI 聊天机器人更友好会提高用户信任，却也可能带来错误和助长阴谋论的传播。提醒设计师在友好度、准确性和安全机制之间取得平衡。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47970597' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.washingtonpost.com/health/2026/04/30/adhd-subtype-extreme-brain-scans/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brain scans reveal 3 ADHD subtypes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>脑部成像揭示 ADHD 的三种亚型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47970597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47970597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47970597' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Brain scans reveal three ADHD subtypes, suggesting a more nuanced neurobiological categorization that could guide diagnosis and personalized treatment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>脑部成像显示 ADHD 有三种亚型，提示更细致的神经生物学分型，或有助于诊断和个性化治疗方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47921802' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47921802' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bensimms.moe/postgres-lateral-makes-quite-a-good-dsl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Postgres&#x27;s lateral joins allow for quite the good eDSL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgreSQL 的横向连接让人可以构建相当优秀的 eDSL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47921802' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL&#x27;s lateral joins unlock expressive, embedded DSLs by letting subqueries reference outer columns. The technique can simplify building domain-specific languages inside SQL and enable powerful, composable query patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 的横向连接使子查询能引用外部列，从而支持更具表达力的嵌入式 DSL。此技术有助于在 SQL 内部构建领域特定语言，并实现更强大、可组合的查询模式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47942353' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47942353' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lttlabs.com/articles/2026/04/11/apple-studio-display-xdr-display-testing-results' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple CMF (Color-Matching Functions) 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果 CMF 2026：色彩匹配函数评测</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47942353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47942353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47942353' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s CMF 2026 evaluation covers Studio Display XDR color accuracy across lighting conditions, with measurements of color fidelity, gamma, and gamut coverage. The results highlight how Apple calibrates displays for creative work and the importance of precise color science in consumer hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apple 的 CMF 2026 测评聚焦 Studio Display XDR 的色彩还原在不同照明条件下的表现，涵盖色彩保真度、伽马和色域覆盖等指标。报告强调苹果在创作工作对色彩准确性的需求上的校准，以及色彩科学在消费级硬件中的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47930003' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(345) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260427-00/?p=112271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Consequences of passing too few register parameters to a C function</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>向 C 函数传递过少寄存器参数的后果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47930003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47930003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47930003' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Passing too few register parameters to a C function can corrupt the call stack and crash programs due to ABI mismatches. The piece walks through why keeping call conventions aligned matters and how to diagnose such issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向 C 函数传递过少寄存器参数会因调用约定不匹配而破坏调用栈、导致程序崩溃。文中解释了为何保持调用约定一致性的重要性以及如何排查相关问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pressbooks-dev.oer.hawaii.edu/epet302/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Guide to CubeSat Mission and Bus Design</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47906666' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CubeSats democratize access to space by requiring compact, modular design. This guide covers mission planning and bus subsystems like power, attitude control, and communications, helping students and researchers craft affordable space experiments.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47992446' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47992446' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newsonjapan.com/article/149075.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Care homes and hotels in Japan shut as expansion strategy unravels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本养老院与酒店在扩张策略瓦解后关闭</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47992446' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Care homes and hotels in Japan face closures as aggressive expansion plans unravel, signaling strain across service and real estate sectors. Demographic headwinds, labor shortages, and capital constraints undercut growth bets. The development raises questions for operators and investors about sustainable models in an aging society.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本部分养老院与酒店在扩张计划瓦解后宣布关闭，反映服务业与地产领域的压力。人口老龄化带来的挑战、劳动力短缺和资金约束削弱了扩张预期。此情形引发对在老龄化社会中可持续商业模式的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i2.ytimg.com/vi/SLn2VjV3Jko/mqdefault.jpg' data-full='https://i2.ytimg.com/vi/SLn2VjV3Jko/mqdefault.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Joby Aviation 在纽约启动电动空中出租车示范，首场在肯尼迪机场的历史性飞行标志着城市空中出行的实际测试。示范将评估飞行性能、安全性及未来商用的监管路径。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/duane1024/l123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>L123: A Lotus 1-2-3–style terminal spreadsheet with modern Excel compatibility</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47926480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47926480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>L123 recreates Lotus 1-2-3-style terminal spreadsheets with modern Excel compatibility, offering a lightweight, text-based workflow for quick data entry and retro computing nostalgia. It provides a bridge for users who want a fast terminal UI while still leveraging Excel import/export features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>L123 将 Lotus 1-2-3 风格的终端电子表格与现代 Excel 兼容性结合，提供轻量级、文本界面的快速数据处理体验，兼具复古计算的乐趣。它让偏好终端工作流的用户仍然能够使用 Excel 的导入导出功能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(359) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aruljohn.com/blog/macos-created-tar-files-linux-errors/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tar Files Created on macOS Display Errors When Extracting on Linux (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 创建的 tar 文件在 Linux 解压时显示错误（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47961208' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes compatibility issues where tar archives created on macOS fail to extract cleanly on Linux due to BSD vs GNU tar differences, with examples and practical fixes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出在 macOS 上创建的 tar 档在 Linux 上解压时出现兼容性问题，原因涉及 BSD 与 GNU tar 的差异，并给出示例与实际修复方法。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47920214' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discovery.kaust.edu.sa/en/article/26858/gallium-oxide-electronics-withstand-extreme-cold/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gallium oxide electronics withstand extreme cold</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>氧化镓电子在极端低温下仍能稳定工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47920214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47920214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47920214' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gallium oxide-based electronics withstand cryogenic temperatures, enabling reliable sensing and processing in extreme cold environments. The study suggests material advantages for aerospace, space equipment, or quantum experiments where conventional semiconductors fail.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>镓氧化物基电子器件在极低温环境下仍能稳定工作，显示出在极寒条件下的可靠性。该研究提示这类材料在航天、极端环境与量子实验等场景具备潜在优势，因为传统半导体在低温下性能受限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discovery.kaust.edu.sa/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Xiaohang-Li_KD_2026_Apr_HH_T1.jpg' data-full='https://discovery.kaust.edu.sa/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/Xiaohang-Li_KD_2026_Apr_HH_T1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Matheus-git/systemd-manager-tui' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Systemd-manager-TUI: A TUI application for managing systemd services</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47995649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47995649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Systemd-manager-TUI is a terminal UI for managing systemd services. It provides easy start/stop, enable/disable, status checks, and log viewing from a compact CLI interface. Designed as a lightweight alternative for headless or constrained environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Systemd-manager-TUI 是一个用于管理 systemd 服务的文本用户界面。它通过紧凑的 CLI 界面提供启停、启用/禁用、状态查看和日志查看等功能。面向无头或资源受限的环境，旨在成为一个轻量替代工具。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47988441' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a hidden debug mode in a cycling hardware device, revealing how debugging access can unlock advanced capabilities while risking stability. The piece prompts a reflection on balancing developer access and product safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示了一款自行车设备中的隐藏调试模式，显示开发者访问可开启高级功能，同时也可能影响稳定性，引发对开发者权限与产品安全的权衡讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47992802' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47992802' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fshot.org/techzone/the-algorithm-knows.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI, Intimacy, and the Data You Never Meant to Share</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI、亲密关系与你本来不想分享的数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47992802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47992802' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how AI systems handle intimate data and the risks of unintentional sharing, including prompts, training inputs, and inferred details. It advocates privacy-by-design and user controls to mitigate exposure in conversational AI. The piece underscores growing tension between personalization and personal privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>审视 AI 系统如何处理亲密数据及可能未曾打算分享的信息的风险，包括输入提示、训练数据与推断结果。主张从隐私设计出发，强化用户控制以降低对话式 AI 的数据暴露。强调个性化服务与个人隐私之间日益紧张的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fshot.org/images/fs-hot-blocky-hd.webp' data-full='https://fshot.org/images/fs-hot-blocky-hd.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47921377' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47921377' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/serverless-dna/tendril' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tendril – a self-extending agent that builds and registers its own tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tendril —— 一个自我扩展的代理，能构建并注册自己的工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921377' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47921377' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tendril is a self-extending agent that can build and register its own tools, enabling autonomous expansion of capabilities without separate human setups. The project spotlights the potential—and the governance challenges—of agents that bootstrap their own toolchains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tendril 是一个能够自我扩展、构建并注册自身工具的代理，能在无需额外人工配置的情况下扩展能力。该项目凸显了自我引导工具链的潜力与治理挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/33eebaea4552f629e622a53927046492ee5b43ab79ddcac18ec3af6f6e787ccb/serverless-dna/tendril' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/33eebaea4552f629e622a53927046492ee5b43ab79ddcac18ec3af6f6e787ccb/serverless-dna/tendril' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47916964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47916964' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Details the community-driven port of Notepad++ to macOS, covering compatibility adjustments, performance considerations, and licensing questions. It illustrates how community projects extend classic tools beyond their native ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述 Notepad++ 面向 macOS 的社区移植项目，涵盖兼容性调整、性能考量和许可问题，展示社区项目如何将经典工具带入新的生态系统。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(374) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://computer.rip/2026-04-26-voice-modems.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Voice Modems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>语音调制解调器</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47916103' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on voice modems traces their role in early digital communications and why modern networks migrated away from them. It highlights the evolution of dial-up-era tech and its lasting legacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾语音调制解调器在早期数字通信中的作用，以及为何现代网络逐步摆脱它们。揭示拨号上网时代技术的演变与持久影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47963163' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47963163' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(376) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/ai-artificial-intelligence/920401/gen-z-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The More Young People Use AI, the More They Hate It</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年轻人越用 AI 越讨厌它</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47963163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47963163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47963163' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gen Z&#x27;s growing use of AI correlates with rising dissatisfaction, driven by privacy concerns, output quality, and perceived hype vs. reality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gen Z 越来越多地使用 AI，却越发对其感到厌烦，担忧隐私、输出质量和炒作与现实的差距。文章探讨这对产品设计、教育与监管的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/STK_414_AI_J-copy.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/04/STK_414_AI_J-copy.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47961934' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47961934' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(377) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/the-science-behind-honeys-eternal-shelf-life-1218690/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Science Behind Honey&#x27;s Eternal Shelf Life (2013)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蜂蜜永恒保质期背后的科学（2013）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47961934' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains why honey can last essentially indefinitely when stored properly: very low water activity, acidic pH, and natural antimicrobial compounds hinder microbial growth. It also notes practical caveats like crystallization and storage conditions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蜂蜜在妥善储存时几乎可无限保质的科学原因在于水活性极低、酸性环境和天然抗菌成分，文中也提及会结晶和存储条件对保质期的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/EatmTIW2gw9eXANL9RF6CGbgp8o=/fit-in/1600x0/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer/Surprising-Science-honey.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/EatmTIW2gw9eXANL9RF6CGbgp8o=/fit-in/1600x0/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer/Surprising-Science-honey.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47944931' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47944931' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(378) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/abs/10.1145/3767295.3803591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Low-Compilation-Cost Register Allocation in LLVM-Based Binary Translation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 LLVM 基于的二进制翻译中实现低编译成本的寄存器分配</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47944931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47944931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47944931' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers present a register allocation strategy for LLVM-based binary translation that minimizes compilation cost while maintaining high runtime performance. The approach reduces the overhead of dynamic translation by improving spill code efficiency and register reuse, enabling faster on-the-fly translation in emulators and VMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究提出一种在基于 LLVM 的二进制翻译中低编译成本的寄存器分配策略，在保持高运行时性能的同时减少编译开销。该方法通过提高溢出代码效率和寄存器复用来降低动态翻译的开销，适用于仿真器和虚拟机中的即时翻译。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After two decades with WordPress, the author breaks away, citing plugin bloat, maintenance burden, and a desire for a more modern stack. The piece outlines a migration strategy, covering content preservation, SEO implications, and choosing a new framework or static-site approach. It serves as a case study for long-running sites reconsidering their tech stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 WordPress 持续使用了二十年后，作者决定告别，原因包括插件臃肿、维护负担与希望采用更现代的技术栈。文章给出迁移策略，涵盖内容保留、SEO 影响以及选择新框架或静态站点方案。这为长期站点重新评估技术栈提供了一个案例研究。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47973635' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dijkstra offers a thoughtful take on APL, weighing its expressive power against readability and maintainability. The letter context sheds light on how APL influenced early language design and the ongoing debate between conciseness and clarity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>迪克斯特拉对 APL 的表达力与可读性之间的权衡提出独到见解，兼具赞赏与批评。此信件为早期编程语言设计的历史提供了洞见，展示简洁性与清晰度之间的张力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kannauj 的香匠世代以季风气息制成 mitti attar 的传统历史悠久。文章探寻这种香水工艺、历史背景，以及嗅觉技艺如何成为一种与科技相邻的文化。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47952773' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47952773' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-026-71526-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Direct electrochemical black coffee quality appraisal using cyclic voltammetry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>直接用循环伏安法评估黑咖啡品质</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47952773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47952773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47952773' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate that cyclic voltammetry can quantify coffee quality by analyzing oxidation/reduction features linked to roasting level and antioxidant content. The method offers a rapid, non-destructive assay that could standardize quality control in coffee supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示循环伏安法可通过分析氧化还原信号来表征烘焙程度与抗氧化物含量，从而评估咖啡品质。这种快速、非破坏性的方法有望为咖啡供应链带来标准化的质量控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41467-026-71526-5/MediaObjects/41467_2026_71526_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41467-026-71526-5/MediaObjects/41467_2026_71526_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47960778' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.heise.de/en/news/1-4-GW-Huge-battery-storage-at-former-Grohnde-nuclear-power-plant-11277367.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1.4 GW: battery storage at former Grohnde nuclear power plant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>前 Grohnde 核电站 1.4GW 巨型储能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47960778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47960778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47960778' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A massive 1.4 GW battery storage project is planned at the site of the former Grohnde nuclear plant, signaling the shift to grid-scale storage and energy resilience. It shows how decommissioned plants can host renewable storage assets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在前 Grohnde 核电站计划建设 1.4GW 级别的巨型储能项目，标志着对电网规模化储能与能源韧性的重视。退役核电设施正在转为承载可再生储能的潜在资产。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47909979' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47909979' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(400) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hmpcabral.com/2026/04/26/the-fastest-linux-timestamps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The fastest Linux timestamps</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 时间戳之最速</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47909979' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores high-precision timestamps in Linux, including options like monotonic clocks and their performance tradeoffs. The piece helps developers pick the right clock source for latency-sensitive applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 Linux 的高精度时间戳选项（如单调时钟）及其性能权衡，帮助开发者为对延迟敏感的应用选择合适的时钟源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hmpcabral.com/assets/images/vdso/duration.png' data-full='https://www.hmpcabral.com/assets/images/vdso/duration.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(401) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newyorker.com/magazine/2026/04/27/when-your-digital-life-vanishes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When your digital life vanishes</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A meditation on data loss and digital fragility in modern life, urging readers to consider portability, offline backups, and resilient identity management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>反思现代生活中数据丢失的脆弱性，强调数据可携带性、离线备份和健壮的身份管理的重要性。文中给出在互联世界中保护个人信息的实际建议。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47964996' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RightsCon, the major digital human rights conference, was abruptly postponed or canceled, roiling the policy and advocacy communities. It signals how external events ripple through tech policy activism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RightsCon 作为全球主要数字人权会议突然被取消，冲击了政策与倡导社群。文章探讨可能的原因、对正在进行的数字权利行动的影响，以及对主办方、参与者和赞助方的后续影响，反映出外部事件对科技政策行动的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47947834' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://healthmanagement.org/c/icu/IssueArticle/improving-handovers-by-learning-from-scuderia-ferrari' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Improving ICU handovers by learning from Scuderia Ferrari F1 team</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>向法拉利 F1 团队学习改善 ICU 交接</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47947834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47947834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47947834' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICU handovers could benefit from the discipline of Formula 1 pit teams, applying structured briefings, checklists, and rapid decision loops to reduce miscommunication. The article shows practical steps that translate high-stakes racing rigor into safer patient transitions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 F1 车队的高强度沟通节奏带入 ICU 交接，使用结构化简报、清单和快速决策循环来降低沟通错误。文章给出把高风险竞速领域的严谨移植到患者转运中的可操作做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://healthmanagement.org/bundles/hmnewtheme/img/ICU_LOGO.png' data-full='https://healthmanagement.org/bundles/hmnewtheme/img/ICU_LOGO.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post details scaling LFM2 to 24B parameters with architectural and optimization tweaks to run on practical hardware, illustrating how large open models can be pushed toward real-world use.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47957494' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47957494' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/2026/04/28/continuing-the-story-of-early-dos-development/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft open sources DOS 1.00 on 45th anniversary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软在 DOS 1.00 45 周年之际开源源码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957494' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957494' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47957494' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>On DOS 1.0&#x27;s 45th anniversary, Microsoft released the open-source release of the original operating system code, offering historical insight into the original software and development practices. The release invites researchers and enthusiasts to study early OS design and Microsoft’s archival practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 DOS 1.0 45 周年之际，微软公开了原始操作系统代码的开源版本，为研究早期软件与开发实践提供宝贵资料。此举为研究者与爱好者提供了了解早期操作系统设计与微软存档实践的机会。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/86-DOS-Changelist.png' data-full='https://opensource.microsoft.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/86-DOS-Changelist.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47920599' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47920599' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/04/we-still-dont-have-a-more-precise-value-for-big-g/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We still don&#x27;t have a more precise value for &quot;Big G&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于“大G”值，我们仍未得到更精确的数值</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47920599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47920599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47920599' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physicists still lack a definitive, more precise measurement of the gravitational constant G, with different experiments yielding inconsistent values. The stubborn uncertainty poses a challenge for precision physics and may hint at unidentified systematic errors or new physics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于万有引力常数 G 的精确测量仍缺乏统一的更高精度数值，不同实验给出的结果不一致。这一持续的不确定性冲击精密物理学的基准，也可能指向未识别的系统误差或潜在的新物理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/bigG1-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/bigG1-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47973559' data-tags='Show HN,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47973559' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sitemogging.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Site Mogging</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Site Mogging</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47973559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47973559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47948152' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/roboticist-turned-teacher-eniac-replica' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Roboticist-Turned-Teacher Built a Life-Size Replica of Eniac</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从机器人专家转为教师：打造了 ENIAC 的等身复制品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47948152' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A robotics-trained educator built a life-size replica of ENIAC, reviving the iconic pre-transistor computer for teaching and public engagement. The project shows how hands-on making and robotics skills can illuminate early computing history and inspire students. It also demonstrates value in interdisciplinary approaches to tech education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位具备机器人学背景的教师打造了 ENIAC 的等身复制品，用于教学与公众互动。该项目展示了动手制作与机器人技能如何点亮早期计算机历史并激发学生兴趣，同时凸显跨学科方法在科技教育中的价值。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eetimes.com/what-the-dram-crunch-teaches-us-about-system-design/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DRAM Crunch: Lessons for System Design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DRAM 短缺：对系统设计的启示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47933359' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DRAM supply constraints are forcing designers to rethink memory hierarchy, capacity planning, and power budgets. The piece distills practical lessons for system architects as memory costs rise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DRAM 供给紧张迫使系统设计者重新审视内存层次结构、容量规划和功耗预算。文中为系统架构师提炼了在内存成本上升背景下的实用经验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为 QDay Prize 从一开始就注定失败——激励错位、标准不清导致可预测的失败。该批评为设计有野心的科技奖项计划提供了经验教训。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://skysedge.com/telecom/RUSP/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Rotary Un-Smartphone (2023)</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Rotary Un-Smartphone project revisits a rotary dial experience as a minimal, privacy-focused device, eschewing modern apps for a tactile, slower form of communication. It examines design choices that prioritize user agency and a lighter digital footprint.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过重新回到拨号轮体验，该项目打造了一款低隐私、极简的设备，放弃大量应用，强调更具触觉和节奏的沟通方式。设计选择聚焦于提升用户自主性与减轻数字足迹。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scottaaronson.blog/?p=9718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scott Aaronson on quantum: &quot;Will you heed my warnings NOW?&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Scott Aaronson 关于量子：‘现在你们会听我的警告吗？’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47958942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47958942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47958942' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scott Aaronson reiterates urgent warnings about the long-term risks and timelines of quantum computing, urging policymakers, researchers, and the public to take precautions now. He discusses plausible futures, potential hazards, and strategies to mitigate existential risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scott Aaronson 再次强调量子计算的长期风险与时间表，敦促政策制定者、研究者和公众立即采取应对措施。他讨论了可能的未来情景、潜在危害及减轻生存风险的策略。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Brent Crude surged to 119.56 per barrel, hitting a fresh high, reflecting supply concerns and geopolitical factors. The move has broad implications for energy markets, inflation trajectories, and tech supply chains sensitive to oil prices.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canva apologizes after its AI tool replaces &#x27;Palestine&#x27; in designs, highlighting the need for guardrails and content moderation in generative tools.</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47985932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47985932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47985932' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A startup claims infrasound waves can suppress kitchen fires, potentially replacing sprinklers; experts express skepticism about effectiveness, safety, and scalability. The topic underscores the long-standing challenge of reliable fire suppression tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家初创公司声称低频声波可扑灭厨房火灾，理论上或可替代喷淋系统；专家对效果、安全性与普适性持怀疑态度。此话题凸显了长期以来对可靠火灾抑制技术的挑战。</p>

</div>

The release signals ongoing maturity of Zulip as an open-source option for structured, threaded collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zulip 12.0 发布，带来新功能和性能提升，改善复杂线程与集成场景下的团队协作。此版本体现了 Zulip 作为开源结构化协作工具的持续成熟。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47943190' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frontporchrepublic.com/2025/09/when-the-internet-was-a-place/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When the Internet Was a Place</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互联网曾是一个场所</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47943190' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflection on how the early Internet felt as a shared space, contrasting today&#x27;s large platforms with the old days of independent hosts, blogs, and IRC. The piece argues that the sense of a public, navigable &#x27;place&#x27; shaped technical culture and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾早期互联网作为共同场所的体验，比较当今以大平台为主的格局与昔日的独立主机、博客和 IRC 的时代。文章探讨这种“公共场所”的感觉如何塑造了技术文化与治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.frontporchrepublic.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/5321590647_afe4692f53_b.jpg' data-full='https://www.frontporchrepublic.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/5321590647_afe4692f53_b.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47904615' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47904615' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47904615' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay exploring how occult practices and esoteric magic were marketed and distributed through mail-order channels, reflecting on the cultural and historical context of information networks. The piece ties postal history to the spread of belief and mysticism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇探讨在邮购渠道中传播的秘术与神秘学的历史现象，揭示信息网络如何塑造信仰与传播。文章将邮政史与神秘主义联系起来，展现文化与传播的交汇。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(427) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Pathos0925/SatoshiGuesser' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SatoshiGuesser – Roll for Bitcoin</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SatoshiGuesser – 为比特币来一把的掷骰子游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47964897' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47964897' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A GitHub project that gamifies Bitcoin odds by rolling dice to simulate outcomes related to Satoshi, the smallest unit of BTC. The project blends crypto enthusiasm with probability play and can serve as a playful educational tool for understanding randomness in crypto systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 GitHub 项目，通过掷骰子来模拟与比特币最小单位聪相关的赔率，玩味十足。该项目将对加密货币的热情与概率游戏结合，是理解区块链系统中随机性的有趣教育工具。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(428) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SHRDLU' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shrdlu</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SHRDLU：早期自然语言理解程序</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47950156' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SHRDLU was an early AI system from the 1960s that interpreted natural language in a blocks world, laying groundwork for later dialogue and planning systems. It remains a foundational milestone in AI history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SHRDLU 是20世纪60年代的早期自然语言理解系统，能在方块世界中理解指令并执行计划，为后来的对话系统和规划研究奠定基础。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(429) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://interfaze.ai/blog/introducing-structured-output-benchmark' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A new benchmark for testing LLMs for deterministic outputs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用于测试 LLM 确定性输出的新基准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47950283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47950283' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The benchmark from Interfaze AI targets deterministic outputs for LLMs, offering a standardized way to measure and compare how consistently models reply to identical prompts. It helps engineers reduce nondeterminism in production systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Interfaze AI 推出的新基准专注于评估大模型输出的确定性，为在同一输入下模型回复的一致性提供标准化度量，帮助工程师降低生产环境中的非确定性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://interfaze.ai/api/og?title=Introducing%20SOB%3A%20A%20Multi-Source%20Structured%20Output%20Benchmark%20for%20LLMs' data-full='https://interfaze.ai/api/og?title=Introducing%20SOB%3A%20A%20Multi-Source%20Structured%20Output%20Benchmark%20for%20LLMs' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind 提出解耦的 DiLoCo 架构，提升分布式 AI 训练的弹性与可扩展性，通过解耦各组件提升容错能力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/the-oscars-just-banned-ai-from-winning-acting-and-writing-awards-2000753740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Oscars just banned AI from winning acting and writing awards</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>奥斯卡刚宣布禁止 AI 获得最佳表演与编剧奖项</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47999346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47999346' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Oscars block AI from winning acting and writing awards, reinforcing human-centered categories and prompting studios to rethink AI&#x27;s role in production. It signals a turning point for how AI is treated in mainstream entertainment awards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>奥斯卡宣布禁用 AI 参与最佳表演与最佳编剧等奖项，强调人类作品的优先地位，并促使业界重新评估 AI 在制作中的角色。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47972481' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47972481' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(433) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unherd.com/2026/04/is-ai-the-next-phase-of-evolution/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When Dawkins met Claude – Could this AI be conscious?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当 Dawkins 遇见 Claude：这 AI 会有意识吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47972481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47972481' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Considers whether Claude-like AI could be conscious, using Dawkins’ ideas about biology and evolution to frame the debate. It weighs traditional markers of consciousness against modern AI behavior and capabilities. The piece prompts readers to reflect on what genuine awareness in AI would entail and why it matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过将道金斯的生物与进化观念引入，探讨像 Claude 这样的 AI 是否可能具备意识。对比传统意识标志与当代 AI 行为及能力，思考若 AI 真正具备意识需要具备哪些条件以及这对现实意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unherd.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/image-1.jpg' data-full='https://unherd.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/image-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47966668' data-tags='Data,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47966668' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(434) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/systalyze/utilyze' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Utilyze measures how efficiently your GPU is doing useful work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Utilyze：衡量 GPU 有用工作效率</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47966668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47966668' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47966668' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Utilyze introduces a metric to quantify how much of a GPU&#x27;s work is &#x27;useful&#x27; for real tasks. It separates productive compute from wasted cycles, memory stalls, or idle time to guide kernel choice and hardware planning. The project provides tooling to measure efficiency during runtime across workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Utilyze 引入一个度量，用以量化 GPU 实际有用工作的比例。它将有用的计算与无效循环、内存瓶颈和空闲时间区分开来，帮助优化内核与硬件选型。该项目还提供运行时在不同工作负载下衡量效率的工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7d034eb1338d82acefeb6c298136ec3700cda76378430d3abd615d6749de46df/systalyze/utilyze' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7d034eb1338d82acefeb6c298136ec3700cda76378430d3abd615d6749de46df/systalyze/utilyze' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(436) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/research-highlights/a-grounded-conceptual-model-for-ownership-types-in-rust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A grounded conceptual model for ownership types in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 拥有类型的扎实概念模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47957116' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A grounded conceptual model for Rust&#x27;s ownership types clarifies how borrowing, lifetimes, and safety semantics fit together. It helps developers reason about memory management and concurrency safety beyond formal type-system details.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Rust 的拥有类型提出了一个扎实的概念模型，阐明了借用、生命周期与安全语义如何协同工作，帮助开发者在超越形式类型系统的层面理解内存管理与并发安全。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47972392' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>North Korean defectors face ongoing language hurdles when resettling, including dialect differences, education gaps, and mistranslation in media and official documents. These barriers complicate integration and access to services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>北朝鲜叛逃者在重新安置时面临持续的语言障碍，包括方言差异、教育资源不足，以及媒体和官方文件的翻译误差。这些阻碍了融入与获取服务的过程。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NaN is more than a placeholder; the piece explores how NaN behaves across languages and operations, revealing quirks like comparisons and propagation that can trip developers. Understanding its semantics helps prevent bugs in numerical computation and data pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NaN 不仅是一个占位符，它的行为在不同语言和运算中存在差异，暴露出诸如比较和传播等容易导致 bug 的特性。理解其语义有助于避免数值计算和数据处理中的错误。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47871877' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Orinoco introduces a parallel scavenger for the young generation in V8, enabling faster GC pauses and higher throughput for JavaScript workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Orinoco 为 V8 引入年轻代并行清理器，使 JavaScript 工作负载在垃圾回收时的暂停更短、吞吐更高。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47985553' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47985553' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/science-and-technology/2022/10/21/becoming-a-father-shrinks-your-cerebrum' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Becoming a father shrinks your cerebrum</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成为父亲会让大脑缩小吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47985553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47985553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47985553' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study reports that fatherhood is associated with reduced gray matter in regions tied to social cognition and parenting behavior, suggesting adaptive brain remodeling to support parenting. The findings point to long-lasting neural changes tied to life transitions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现，成为父亲与大脑灰质在与社会认知和育儿行为相关的区域的缩小有关，暗示为适应育儿而发生的脑部重塑。这一结果指向与人生阶段相关的长期神经变化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47982967' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47982967' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(449) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://evilgeniuslabs.ca/blog/the-feed-doesnt-know-you' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The feed doesn&#x27;t know you, and YouTube refuses to let you browse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>信息流不认识你，YouTube 不让你随意浏览</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47982967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47982967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47982967' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that recommendation feeds narrow discovery by shaping what you see, while platforms like YouTube limit unpersonalized browsing experiences. It calls for more transparent algorithms and greater user control over exploration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为信息流通过塑造你所看到的内容来限制探索空间，而像 YouTube 这样的平台往往不提供非个性化的浏览体验。呼吁提高算法透明度，并给予用户更多的探索控制权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://evilgeniuslabs.ca/img/og-default.png' data-full='https://evilgeniuslabs.ca/img/og-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2026 experiment uses a DEC VT-100 terminal to explore retro computing today, highlighting the charm and limits of 1970s hardware. The setup sheds light on minimal interfaces and resilience trade-offs compared to modern user experiences.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的终端像在挖矿一样烧电</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47941517' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47941517' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47941517' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into how modern terminal apps and shells can drain laptop batteries by constant GPU/CPU work, GPU acceleration, and animations, akin to mining.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇博文分析了终端应用和 shell 如何通过持续的 CPU/GPU 负载、动画等导致电池快速耗尽。给出针对 iTerm2 等工具的优化建议，帮助减少耗电。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
