<p class='hn-title'>(452) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mpie.de/5151287/short-circuit-solid-state-batteries' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding the short circuit in solid-state batteries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>理解固态电池中的短路现象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47905761' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Short circuits in solid-state batteries arise from dendrites and interfacial breakdown, with ongoing research into suppressing dendrite growth and stabilizing interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>固态电池的短路常见于枝晶生长和界面失稳，研究重点包括抑制枝晶和稳定界面以提升安全性与寿命。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.siliconimist.com/p/semiconductors-made-in-america' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Semiconductors Were Made in America</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国产半导体如何制造</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47937611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47937611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47937611' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An overview of how semiconductors are made in the United States, covering manufacturing stages, supply-chain constraints, and policy implications that shape where chips are produced. It highlights the push to bring more fabrication domestically to secure critical tech infrastructure and reduce reliance on overseas fabs, with implications for customers and startups relying on domestic supply.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概览美国产半导体制造的各个环节、供应链约束以及影响产地选择的政策因素。文章强调推动本土化晶圆厂建设以增强关键技术基础设施的安全性，并减少对海外晶圆厂的依赖，对依赖国内供应的企业与初创公司影响深远。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive gate explorer for San Francisco International Airport helps travelers locate gates and navigate the terminal more efficiently during layovers or peak periods.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/29/on-the-stand-elon-musk-cant-escape-his-own-tweets/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>On the stand, Elon Musk can&#x27;t escape his own tweets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在法庭上，Elon Musk 无法摆脱自己的推文</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47957100' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Elon Musk faced testimony about past tweets and communications, highlighting how social media activity intersects with legal obligations. The coverage underscores the broader tech- and finance-law implications of high-profile public statements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在法庭听证中，埃隆·马斯克就其以往的推文与发言接受质询，凸显社交媒体活动与法律义务之间的关系。报道强调公众人物言论对科技与金融领域的潜在法律影响。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47903959' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47903959' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(459) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.apnic.net/2026/04/24/revocation-of-x-509-certificates/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Revocation of X.509 Certificates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>X.509证书的吊销机制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47903959' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Certificate revocation remains a tricky pillar of PKI, with CRLs and OCSP often lagging or exposing privacy concerns. The piece argues for adopting short-lived certificates and improved validation to reduce reliance on revocation checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>证书吊销是公钥基础设施中的一个棘手难题，CRL 与 OCSP 常常滞后且可能带来隐私风险。文章主张采用短期有效证书并改进在线验证以降低对吊销的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/2026-04-revocation.png' data-full='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/2026-04-revocation.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47973710' data-tags='Open Source,Politics,Health,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47973710' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(460) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/05/nhs-goes-to-war-against-open-source/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NHS Goes to War Against Open Source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NHS 与开源的一场战争</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47973710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47973710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47973710' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Critiques NHS policies that hinder open-source software adoption, arguing it stifles innovation, vendor neutrality, and security benefits from community-driven tooling. Calls for policy reforms to embrace open-source benefits in healthcare IT.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评 NHS 的做法，认为其对开源软件的限制阻碍创新、供应商中立性以及社区驱动工具的安全收益，呼吁在医疗 IT 中重新评估政策以拥抱开源的潜在好处。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/SDLC-8.webp' data-full='https://shkspr.mobi/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/SDLC-8.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cordouan_Lighthouse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cordouan Lighthouse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科杜昂灯塔</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47948338' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cordouan Lighthouse stands at the mouth of the Gironde estuary as one of France’s oldest and architecturally distinctive lighthouses. Its construction and upkeep illustrate the evolution of maritime signaling and state power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科杜昂灯塔位于吉伦特河口，是法国最古老且建筑风格独特的灯塔之一。其建造与维护反映了航海信号技术与国家权力的演变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/b/b6/Cordouan.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/b/b6/Cordouan.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/enshittification/comments/1srjamd/you_have_to_have_an_account_to_read_imdb_reviews/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You have to have an account to read IMDB reviews now</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47925845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47925845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47925845' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IMDB now requires user accounts to read reviews, reflecting the broader trend of gating access to user-generated content. The change affects casual readers and content ecosystems, potentially reducing discovery and increasing friction for new users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IMDB 现在阅读影评需要登录账户，这体现了对用户生成内容进行门槛化的趋势。此举可能削弱随意浏览的便利性，增加新用户的使用门槛，同时影响平台的内容发现生态。</p>

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47923527' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EFF challenges secrecy in an Eastern District of Texas patent case, arguing transparency is essential for accountability in patent litigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EFF 对德州东区一项专利案中的保密安排提出挑战，主张在专利诉讼中应实现透明与问责。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47899199' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47899199' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(481) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.os2museum.com/wp/mystery-cpuid-bit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mystery Cpuid Bit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>神秘的 CPUID 位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47899199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47899199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47899199' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CPUID flags reveal processor features, but some bits remain undocumented or poorly understood. This note digs into a mystery CPUID bit and why software sometimes encounters ambiguity in low-level detection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CPUID 标志揭示处理器特性，但仍有一些位未被文档清楚描述。本文探讨一个神秘的 CPUID 位及其为何在底层检测中引发困惑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.os2museum.com/wp/wp-content/uploads/2012/02/os2floppy.jpg' data-full='http://www.os2museum.com/wp/wp-content/uploads/2012/02/os2floppy.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47952096' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-47952096' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(482) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.audubon.org/magazine/tennessees-most-historic-hotel-also-home-greatest-duck-tradition' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Historic Tennessee hotel is also home to the greatest duck tradition (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>田纳西历史酒店与其鸭子传统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47952096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47952096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47952096' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The historic Tennessee hotel is famous for its long-running duckling tradition, a quirky hallmark that attracts visitors and preservationists. The feature highlights how small rituals can shape a property&#x27;s identity and branding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这家历史悠久的田纳西酒店因其长期流传的鸭子传统而知名，成为吸引游客与保护者的独特标志。文章强调这种小型仪式如何塑造酒店的身份与品牌。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.audubon.org/sfw_duck-march-antony.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;auto=webp&amp;quality=90&amp;fit=crop&amp;enable=upscale' data-full='https://media.audubon.org/sfw_duck-march-antony.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;auto=webp&amp;quality=90&amp;fit=crop&amp;enable=upscale' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tvheadend is a self-hosted IPTV server project that aggregates live TV, EPG, and DVR capabilities for home media setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tvheadend 是一个自托管的 IPTV 服务器，集成直播电视、节目表与 DVR 功能，适合家庭媒体中心使用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://biohub.org/news/virtual-biology-initiative/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>$500M for Virtual Biology Initiative, Funded by Zuckerbergs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>由扎克伯格家族资助的虚拟生物学计划获 5 亿美元资助</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47970023' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A $500 million commitment to a Virtual Biology Initiative backed by the Zuckerbergs aims to accelerate computational biology, simulations, and virtual experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由扎克伯格家族支持的虚拟生物学计划获5亿美元资助，目标是推动计算生物学、仿真和虚拟实验的发展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(485) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://options-game-polymathrobotics.pythonanywhere.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Startup Equity Adventure Game</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：创业股权探险游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47915274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47915274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47915274' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Showcases a browser-based game simulating startup equity and cap table dynamics, offering a playful lens on fundraising math and founder decisions. It presents an approachable way to explore equity scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示一个基于浏览器的游戏，模拟创业公司股权与股权表的动态，提供对募资算术与创始人决策的趣味化视角，便于理解各种股权情景。</p>
